## צ (id: BDB04078)

\[Str6631] † \[צֶאֱצָא] **n.m.**:[Jb 27:14](https://ref.ly/Job27:14) only pl., issue, offspring, produce (NoZMG 1886, 723 comps. ضِيءضِِيءٌ *origin, root, stock*)—**1\.** *offspring* of men, abs. הַצֶּאֱצָאִים וְהַצְּפִעוֺת [Is 22:24](https://ref.ly/Isa22:24); elsewhere only Is:2, Is:3 and Job: cstr. צֶאֱעָאֵי מֵעֶיְ [Is 48:19](https://ref.ly/Isa48:19) (‖זַרְעְֲ); sf. צֶאֱצָאַי [Jb 31:8](https://ref.ly/Job31:8), צֶאֱצָאֶיְ [5:25](https://ref.ly/Job5:25) (‖זַרְעְֲ), [Is 44:3](https://ref.ly/Isa44:3) (‖ *id.*); חֶאֱחָיָאו [Jb 27:14](https://ref.ly/Job27:14) (‖בָּנָיו); צֶאֱצָאֵיהֶ ם [21:8](https://ref.ly/Job21:8) (‖זַרְעָם) [Is 61:9](https://ref.ly/Isa61:9) (‖ *id.*), [65:23](https://ref.ly/Isa65:23). **2\.** *prpdice* of earth, sf. תֵּבל וְכָל־צֶאֱצָאֶָיהָ [Is 34:1](https://ref.ly/Isa34:1); הָאָרֶץ וְצי [42:5](https://ref.ly/Isa42:5).

> \[Str4161] † I. מוֺצָא **n.m.**:[Ho 6:3](https://ref.ly/Hos6:3) place or act of going forth, issue, export, souce, spring;—abs. ´מ [Jb 28:1](https://ref.ly/Job28:1), [Psalm 75:7](https://ref.ly/Ps75:7); cstr. ´מ [Nu 30:13](https://ref.ly/Num30:13) \+ 9 t.; מֹצָא [Jb 38:27](https://ref.ly/Job38:27), [Dn 9:25](https://ref.ly/Dan9:25); sf. מוֺצָאְֲ [2 S 3:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:25); מֹצָאוֺ [Ho 6:3](https://ref.ly/Hos6:3); מוֺצָאוֺ [Psalm 19:7](https://ref.ly/Ps19:7); pl. cstr. מוֺצָאֵי [Is 41:18](https://ref.ly/Isa41:18) \+ 5 t.; sf. מוֺצָאָיו Ez [43:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:11); מוֺצָאֵיהֶם [Nu 33:2](https://ref.ly/Num33:2), [33:2](https://ref.ly/Num33:2); מוֺצָאֵיהֶן Ez [42:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:11); —**1\.** *a going forth:* **a.** the act, of ´י [Ho 6:3](https://ref.ly/Hos6:3); of the sun \= *rising* [Psalm 19:7](https://ref.ly/Ps19:7) (v. יצא **1 f**); of man [2 S 3:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:25) (opp. מָבוֺא Kt); דָּבָר ´מ [Dn 9:25](https://ref.ly/Dan9:25) *going forth of a command.* **b.** concrete, מוֺצָאֵי גּוֺלָה Ez [12:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:4) *goings forth of* exile, i.e. *those going forth into* exile (in sim.). **c.** *way out, exit:* concrete, of chambers in temple Ez [42:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:11), [43:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:11) (opp. מוֺבָיָאו); מוֺצָאֵי הַמִּקְדָּשׁ Ez [44:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:5) (opp. מְבוֺא הַבַּיִת). **2\.** *that which goes forth:* **a.** *utteranc* of mouth or lips (esp. of solemn or formal speech), ´מוֺצָא פִי־י [Dt 8:3](https://ref.ly/Deut8:3); ´שְפָתַי מ [Je 17:16](https://ref.ly/Jer17:16), [Psalm 89:35](https://ref.ly/Ps89:35); so [Dt 23:24](https://ref.ly/Deut23:24), [Nu 30:13](https://ref.ly/Num30:13) (P). **b.** *export* of horses [1 K 10:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:23) \= [2 Ch 1:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:16). **3\.** *place of going forth:* **a.** *source* or *spring* of water [2 K 2:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:21), [Is 58:11](https://ref.ly/Isa58:11) (in sim.) [Psalm 107:33](https://ref.ly/Ps107:33) (‖נְהָרוֺת), v:[35](https://ref.ly/Ps107:35) (‖אֲגַמ־מים) \= [Is 41:18](https://ref.ly/Isa41:18) (‖ *id.*); מוֺצָא מֵימֵי גִיחוֺן הָעֶלְיוֺן [2 Ch 32:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:30). **b.** *place of departure* of Israel on march Nu [33:2](https://ref.ly/Num33:2), [33:2](https://ref.ly/Num33:2) (P). **c.** מוֺצָא \= east (place of sun's going forth) [Psalm 75:7](https://ref.ly/Ps75:7) (opp. מַעֲרָב); zeugmatically, [65:9](https://ref.ly/Ps65:9) מוֺצָאֵי בֹּקֶר וָעֶרֶב i.e. *the east and the west* (cf. the *two Orients,* Qor 43:37\). **d.** place whence silver comes \= *mine* [Jb 28:1](https://ref.ly/Job28:1); וּלְהַצְמִיחַ מֹצָא רֶשֶׁא [38:27](https://ref.ly/Job38:27) and to cause the *growing\-place* (others, *the growth*) of young grass to sprout.

> \[Str4162] † II. מוֺצָא **n.pr.m. 1\.** son of Caleb by Ephah his concubine [1 Ch 2:46](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:46), LXX Ιωσα(ν), LXXL Μουσα. **2\.** a descendant of Saul [1 Ch 8:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:36), [8:37](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:37) (LXX Μαισα) \= [9:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:42), [9:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:43) LXX Μασσα; LXXL in both Μωσα).

> \[Str4163] † \[מוֺצָאָה] **n.f.** only pl.: **a.** [Mi 5:1](https://ref.ly/Mic5:1) מוֺצָאוֺתָיו (cf. future ruler out of Bethlehem), *his origin*. **b.** [2 K 10:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:27) Qr מוֺצָאוֺת *places of going out to,* i.e. *a privy* (cf. Ar. مَخْرَجٌ; Germ. *Abtritt*), euphemistically for Kt מַחֲרָאוֺת.

> \[Str8444] † \[תּוֺצָאָה] **n.f.** outgoing, extremity, souce (?), escape; only pl.; chiefly P and late;—abs. תוֺצָאוֺת [Psalm 68:21](https://ref.ly/Ps68:21); cstr. תוֺצְאוֺת [Pr 4:23](https://ref.ly/Prov4:23) \+ [Jos 18:19](https://ref.ly/Josh18:19) Qr (Kt תוצאותיו); תּוֺצְאֹת [Nu 34:8](https://ref.ly/Num34:8), Ez [48:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:30); תֹּצְאוֺת [Jos 15:4](https://ref.ly/Josh15:4) \+ 2 t.; sf. תּוֺצְאֹתָיו [Nu 34:4](https://ref.ly/Num34:4) \+ 3 t.; תֹּצְאֹתָיו [Jos 15:7](https://ref.ly/Josh15:7) \+ 8 t. \+ [16:3](https://ref.ly/Josh16:3) Qr (Kt תצאתו); תּוֺצְאוֺתָם [1 Ch 5:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:16); —**1\.** *outgoing, extremity* of border of territory [Jos 16:3](https://ref.ly/Josh16:3), [17:18](https://ref.ly/Josh17:18) (both JE), elsewhere P: [Nu 34:4](https://ref.ly/Num34:4), [34:5](https://ref.ly/Num34:5), [34:8](https://ref.ly/Num34:8), [34:9](https://ref.ly/Num34:9), [34:12](https://ref.ly/Num34:12), [Jos 15:4](https://ref.ly/Josh15:4), [15:7](https://ref.ly/Josh15:7), [15:11](https://ref.ly/Josh15:11), [16:8](https://ref.ly/Josh16:8), [17:9](https://ref.ly/Josh17:9), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Josh18:12), [18:14](https://ref.ly/Josh18:14), [18:19](https://ref.ly/Josh18:19), [19:14](https://ref.ly/Josh19:14), [19:22](https://ref.ly/Josh19:22), [19:20](https://ref.ly/Josh19:20), [19:33](https://ref.ly/Josh19:33); *outskirts* of city Ez [48:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:20), cf. [1 Ch 5:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:16). **2\.** חַיִּים ´ת [Pr 4:23](https://ref.ly/Prov4:23) (? read מוֺצְאוֺת) *sources of life.* **3\.** ´לַמָּוֶת ת [Psalm 68:21](https://ref.ly/Ps68:21) *escapes from* (lit. *for,* i.e. *in view of*) *death*.

## צאן (id: BDB07978)

צאן (√ of foll.; DlPr 87 f.; HWB cp. As. *ṣênu,* adj., *good, docile;* Thes (cf. LagBN 136\) cp. יָצָא \[as πρόβατον from προβαίνω]; As. *ṣênu,* \= BH צאֹן, and so NH *id.,* MI31 צאן; Ar. ضَأْنٌ; *id.;* TelAm *ṣunu* is Canaanitish acc. to ZimZA vi (1891\). 156; Aram. עָנָא, ܥܳܢܳܐ, and perhaps Palm. ענא).

> \[Str6629] צאֹן:273 **n. coll. f.**:[Gn 30:36](https://ref.ly/Gen30:36) (poss. m. v:[39](https://ref.ly/Gen30:39) a \+, KöSynt. § 247 g, but dub., v:[39](https://ref.ly/Gen30:39) b c. vb. fpl., as usually; in [1 S 15:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:14) join הַוָּה with קוֺל; v. further AlbrZAW xv (1895\), 316 ff.) small cattle, sheep and goats, flock, flocks;—´צ abs. [Gn 4:2](https://ref.ly/Gen4:2) \+ ; cstr. [29:9](https://ref.ly/Gen29:9) \+ ; sf. צאֹנוֺ [4:4](https://ref.ly/Gen4:4) \+; צאֹנֵנוּ [Ex 10:9](https://ref.ly/Exod10:9), צאֹונֵוּ [Psalm 144:13](https://ref.ly/Ps144:13); as pl. צאֹנוֺ [Ne 10:37](https://ref.ly/Neh10:37) van d. H. Gi (not Baer; very late, si vera l.), etc.;—**1\.** lit. **a.** *small cattle,* usu. of sheep and goats in one flock (RobBR i. 477\) [Gn 30:31](https://ref.ly/Gen30:31), [30:32](https://ref.ly/Gen30:32) \+ ; sheep specified also [21:28](https://ref.ly/Gen21:28) (´כִּבְשת הַצּ), [31:28](https://ref.ly/Gen31:28) (´אֵילֵי צ) \+; sheep only [1 S 25:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:2) (\+ עִוּים); goats specified [Gn 27:9](https://ref.ly/Gen27:9), [Je 50:8](https://ref.ly/Jer50:8); ´צ as yielding (1\) animals for food [Am 6:4](https://ref.ly/Amos6:4) (בָּרִים), [1 S 14:32](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:32), [2 S 12:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:4), Ne 5:l8 \+ ; מַאְַבָל ´צ [Psalm 44:12](https://ref.ly/Ps44:12) (in sim.); (2\) wool [Gn 31:19](https://ref.ly/Gen31:19) \+ (v. \[נָּזַז], נֵּז); (3\) milk, ´חְַלֵב צ [Dt 32:14](https://ref.ly/Deut32:14) (? goatsrs milk, cf. ## צָבָא (id: BDB07982)

\[Str6633] † \[צָבָא] **vb. wage war, serve** (Sab. צַבא *wage war with,* also n. *army, campaign* HomChrest. 125 CISiv. 299, 1\. 2 DHMVOJ i.28; Eth. ጸብአ፡ ( \> ፀብአ፡) *wage war;* As. *ṣâbu, man, soldier;* NöZMG xl (1886\), 726 al. cp. (on account of Sab. Eth. חַ) Ar. ضَبَأَ *conceal oneself,* hence *lie in wait;* this is phonetically suitable, but better in mng. would be صَبَأَ *go* or *come forth* (against one), etc., so Thes LagBN 21; Frä232 cp. صَبِىٌّ [Ex 38:8](https://ref.ly/Exod38:8), [Zc 14:12](https://ref.ly/Zech14:12); *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִצְבְּאוּ [Nu 31:7](https://ref.ly/Num31:7); *Inf. cstr.* לִצְבֹּא [Is 31:4](https://ref.ly/Isa31:4); לִצְבאֹ [Nu 4:23](https://ref.ly/Num4:23), [8:24](https://ref.ly/Num8:24); *Pt* pl. צֹבְאִים [Nu 31:42](https://ref.ly/Num31:42), [Is 29:7](https://ref.ly/Isa29:7), [29:8](https://ref.ly/Isa29:8); sf. צֹבֶיהָ [Is 29:7](https://ref.ly/Isa29:7) (Ges§ 75 oo; Di צָרֶיהָ); fpl. צֹבְאֹת [Ex 38:8](https://ref.ly/Exod38:8); צֹבְאוֺת [1 S 2:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:22); —**1\.** *wage war, fight,* c. עַל *against,* [Nu 31:7](https://ref.ly/Num31:7) (P) [Is 29:7](https://ref.ly/Isa29:7), [29:8](https://ref.ly/Isa29:8), [31:4](https://ref.ly/Isa31:4), [Zc 14:12](https://ref.ly/Zech14:12), c. sf. [Is 29:7](https://ref.ly/Isa29:7) (?); abs. [Nu 31:42](https://ref.ly/Num31:42)(P). **2\.** *serve* at sacred tent, Levites, c. acc. צָבָא [Nu 4:23](https://ref.ly/Num4:23), [8:24](https://ref.ly/Num8:24) (P); women [Ex 38:8](https://ref.ly/Exod38:8), [38:8](https://ref.ly/Exod38:8) (P) [1 S 2:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:22). **Hiph.** *Pt.* ´הַמַּעְבִּא אֶת־עַם וגו [2 K 25:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:19) \= [Je 52:25](https://ref.ly/Jer52:25) *muster.*.

> I. חבב (√ of foll.; cf. As. *ṣumbu* (\=\**ṣubbu*), *cart;* Targum צִיבָּא *low, covered wagon*).

> \[Str6632] † I. צָב **n.\[m.]** litter ;—abs. ´ח, in עֶגְלֹת צָב [Nu 7:3](https://ref.ly/Num7:3), prob. *litter\-wagons,* i.e. wagons covered, like palanquin; pl. צַבִּים [Is 66:20](https://ref.ly/Isa66:20) *litters* (on Vrss cf. DINu).

> II. חבב (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. ضَبَّ *cleave to ground;* NH חָב \= BH, Syr. ܥܒܐ in Lexx; Ar. ضَبٌّ is a large lizard, v. esp. SeetzenReisen iii. 436 ff.).

> \[Str6632] † II. צָב **n.\[m.]** lizard, as unclean, [Lv 11:29](https://ref.ly/Lev11:29).

> \[Str6639] † \[צָבֶה] **adj.** swelling, swollen;—f. צָבָה [Nu 5:21](https://ref.ly/Num5:21) (as foregoing).

> II. חבה (√ of foll.; meaning dub.; DlPr 159 Buhl al. cp. Ar. صبا *lean, incline* (esp. III.), fig. *incline* toward (إِلَي), *yearn* for, As. *ṣabû*, Aram. חְבָא ܨܒܐ, all *desire*, etc.).

(Judah), צְבִי הִיא לְכָל־הָאֲרָצוֺת Ez [20:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:6), [20:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:15), נּוֺיִם נַחֲלַת אְבִי צִבְאוֺת [Je 3:19](https://ref.ly/Jer3:19) (v. supr.) *heritage of the beauty of beauties of the nations,* i.e. most beauteous heritage; ´הַצּ esp. of Jerus. and temple [Dn 8:9](https://ref.ly/Dan8:9) (Bev), cf. ´אֶרֶץ הַצּ [11:16](https://ref.ly/Dan11:16), [11:41](https://ref.ly/Dan11:41) (v. ## צָבַט (id: BDB08001)

\[Str6642] † \[צָבַט] **vb.** reach, hold out, to (ל Pers.) (prop. *grasp, hold,* so NH (rare), בֵּית הַצְּבִיטָה *handle* (of jug); Ar. ضَبَطَ *hold firmly, seize;* Eth. ፀበጠ፡ *grasp firmly*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִּצְבָּט־לָהּ [Ru 2:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:14) (acc. rei).

* **Associated Passages:** Ruth 2:14

\[Str6651] † \[צָבַר] **vb.** heap up (NH *id.;* Aram. צְבַר Syr. ܨܒܰܪ is *prate, chatter, rave;* Ar ضَبَرَ is *collect, bind together;* cf. further Dozy);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִצְבֹּר [Gn 41:35](https://ref.ly/Gen41:35), [Psalm 39:7](https://ref.ly/Ps39:7); 3 fs. וַתִּצְבָּ־ר [Zc 9:3](https://ref.ly/Zech9:3), etc.;—*heap up*, in great quantity: c. acc. corn [Gn 41:35](https://ref.ly/Gen41:35), [41:49](https://ref.ly/Gen41:49) (E), dead frogs [Ex 8:10](https://ref.ly/Exod8:10) (J), dust Hb [1:10](https://ref.ly/Hab1:10), silver like dust [Zc 9:3](https://ref.ly/Zech9:3), [Jb 27:16](https://ref.ly/Job27:16), cf. (obj. om.) [Psalm 39:7](https://ref.ly/Ps39:7).

## צדק (id: BDB08022)

צדק (√of foll.; NH, Aram. in deriv.; cf. Ar. صَدَقَ *speak the truth* (also صَدْقٌ *hard, even, straight, perfect*); Sab. צדק *just,* epith. of king MordtmZMG xxx (1876\), 37 HomChrest. 125; usu. *excellent* DHMZMG xxix (1875\), 595, 590 CISiv. p. 176; also verb *favour, endow* (one with something) CISiv. > \[Str6663] †\[צָדֵק, צָדֹק] **vb. denom.** be just, righteous;—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. צָֽדְקָה Gn [38:26](https://ref.ly/Gen38:26); 2 ms. צָדַקְתָּ [Jb 33:12](https://ref.ly/Job33:12), [35:7](https://ref.ly/Job35:7); etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִצְדַּק [Jb 9:2](https://ref.ly/Job9:2) \+; יִצְדָּק [Jb 4:17](https://ref.ly/Job4:17), [11:2](https://ref.ly/Job11:2); 3 fpl. תִּצְדַּקְנָה Ez [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52) (LXX Co תְּצַדְּקִין, not Berthol Toy Krae), etc ;—**1\.** *have a just cause, be in the right,* [Jb 9:15](https://ref.ly/Job9:15), [9:20](https://ref.ly/Job9:20), [13:18](https://ref.ly/Job13:18), [34:5](https://ref.ly/Job34:5); in complaint [Jb 33:12](https://ref.ly/Job33:12); c. מִן Pers. [Gn 38:26](https://ref.ly/Gen38:26) (J; of Tamar). **2\.** *be justified,* in one's plea [Jb 11:2](https://ref.ly/Job11:2); c. עִם (man with God) [9:2](https://ref.ly/Job9:2), [25:4](https://ref.ly/Job25:4); ´בי [Is 45:25](https://ref.ly/Isa45:25); by witnesses [43:9](https://ref.ly/Isa43:9); by acquittal [Psalm 143:2](https://ref.ly/Ps143:2), [Is 43:26](https://ref.ly/Isa43:26); by condemnation of opponent [Jb 40:8](https://ref.ly/Job40:8). **3\.** *be just:* of God, in his government, in charging with sin [Psalm 51:6](https://ref.ly/Ps51:6); of ´מִשְׁפְּטֵי־י [19:10](https://ref.ly/Ps19:10). **4\.** *be just, righteous,* in conduct and character: of men [Jb 10:15](https://ref.ly/Job10:15), [15:14](https://ref.ly/Job15:14), [22:3](https://ref.ly/Job22:3), [35:7](https://ref.ly/Job35:7), c. מִן comp., [Jb 4:17](https://ref.ly/Job4:17) (more than God; Dr *at God's hand,* מִן **2 d**), Ez [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52) (\< LXX Co **Pi.**). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. וְנִצְדַּק קֹדֶשׁ consec. [Dn 8:14](https://ref.ly/Dan8:14) *the holy place shall be put right,* in a right condition (MartiKau Buhl SS; Bev Dr MartiComm. *be justified,* its cause vindicated). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. צִדְּקָה [Je 3:11](https://ref.ly/Jer3:11); *Impf.* 2 fs תְּצַדְּקִי Ez [16:51](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:51); *Inf. cstr.* sf. צַדְּקֶָָ֑ [Jb 33:32](https://ref.ly/Job33:32); חַדֶּקְתֵּח (Ges§ 52 p) Ez [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52); צַדְּקוֺ [Jb 32:2](https://ref.ly/Job32:2); —*justify:* עַל צַדְּקוֺ נַפְשׁוֺ מֵאֱלֹהִים [Jb 32:2](https://ref.ly/Job32:2) *because he justified himself rather than God* (cf. **Qal 1**); *make to appear righteous* [Je 3:11](https://ref.ly/Jer3:11), Ez [16:51](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:51), [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52) (all c. מִן comp.); חָפַצְתִּי צַדְּקֶָָ֑ [Jb 33:32](https://ref.ly/Job33:32) *I desire to justify thee* (in thy plea, cf. **Qal 2**). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 1 S. sf. הִצְדַּקְתִּיו [2 S 15:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:4); 3 mpl. הִצְדִּיקוּ [Dt 25:1](https://ref.ly/Deut25:1); *Impf.* 3 ms. יַצְדִּיק [Is 53:11](https://ref.ly/Isa53:11); 1 S. אַצְדִּיק [Ex 23:7](https://ref.ly/Exod23:7), [Jb 27:5](https://ref.ly/Job27:5); *Inf. cstr.* הַצְדִּיק [1 K 8:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:32) \= [2 Ch 6:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:23); *Imv.* mpl. הַצְדִּיקוּ [Psalm 82:3](https://ref.ly/Ps82:3); *Pt.* מַצְדִּיק [Pr 17:15](https://ref.ly/Prov17:15), etc.;—**1\.** *do justice,* in administering law [2 S 15:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:4), [Psalm 82:3](https://ref.ly/Ps82:3). **2\.** *declare righteous, justify,* c. acc. צַדִּיק(ה) [Dt 25:1](https://ref.ly/Deut25:1), [1 K 8:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:32) \= [2 Ch 6:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:23); רָשָׁע [Ex 23:7](https://ref.ly/Exod23:7) (E) [Is 5:23](https://ref.ly/Isa5:23), [Pr 17:15](https://ref.ly/Prov17:15); *Justify* accusers, by recognizing charge as just [Jb 27:5](https://ref.ly/Job27:5). **3\.** *justify, vindicate the cause of, save,* c. acc., [Is 50:8](https://ref.ly/Isa50:8) (of God), c. לְ of obj. [Is 53:11](https://ref.ly/Isa53:11) (of servant of ´י). **4\.** *make righteous, turn to righteousness,* [Dn 12:3](https://ref.ly/Dan12:3), cf. Aboth [5:26](https://ref.ly/Dan5:26), [5:27](https://ref.ly/Dan5:27) Bev. **Hithp.** *Impf.* 1 pl. נִצְטַדָּק [Gn 44:16](https://ref.ly/Gen44:16) (J) how *shall we justify ourselves,* clear ourselves from suspicion?

## צָהֵב (id: BDB08029)

\[Str6668] †\[צָהֵב, צָהֹב, cf. LagBN 31] **vb.** gleam (NH *id.* (of face, bronze, etc.); Ar. صَهِبَ *be red*);—**Hoph.** *Pt.* נְחשֶׁת מֻצְהָב [Ezr 8:27](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:27) *polished bronze* (so NH and\-appar. Hebraism \- Targum [2 Ch 4:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:16)).

> \[Str6669] †צָהֹב **adj.** gleaming, yellow (of hair);—´שֵעָר צ [Lv 13:30](https://ref.ly/Lev13:30), [13:32](https://ref.ly/Lev13:32), [13:36](https://ref.ly/Lev13:36) (opp. שָׁחֹר ´ש v:[31](https://ref.ly/Lev13:31), v:[37](https://ref.ly/Lev13:37)).

* **Associated Passages:** Leviticus 13:36; Ezra 8:27; Leviticus 13:37; Leviticus 13:31; 2 Chronicles 4:16; Leviticus 13:30; Leviticus 13:32


## I צָהַל (id: BDB08031)

\[Str6670] † I. \[צָהַל] **vb.** neigh, cry shrilly (Ar. صَهَلَ *neigh,* Syr ܨܗܰܠ *id.;* Targum [Est 8:15](https://ref.ly/Esth8:15) \= BH);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. צָֽהֲלָה [Est 8:15](https://ref.ly/Esth8:15); 3 pl. צָֽהֲלוּ [Is 24:14](https://ref.ly/Isa24:14); *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִצְהָלוּ [Je 5:8](https://ref.ly/Jer5:8); 2 fs. תצהלי [50:11](https://ref.ly/Jer50:11) Kt, Qr 2 mpl. תִּצְהֲלוּ and so Vrss; *Imv.* fs. צַהֲלִי [Is 10:30](https://ref.ly/Isa10:30) \+; mpl. צַהֲלוּ [Je 31:7](https://ref.ly/Jer31:7); —**1\.** *neigh,* of men under fig. of stallions; c. אֶל *unto* (in desire) [Je 5:8](https://ref.ly/Jer5:8), of profligate Judaeans; abs. [50:11](https://ref.ly/Jer50:11) of arrogant Chaldeans. **2\.** *cry shrilly,* in distress [Is 10:30](https://ref.ly/Isa10:30) (c. adv. acc. קוֺלֵךְ); usu. joy, praise (‖רָנַן) [12:6](https://ref.ly/Isa12:6), [54:1](https://ref.ly/Isa54:1), c. ב *at, over,* [Je 31:7](https://ref.ly/Jer31:7), [Is 24:14](https://ref.ly/Isa24:14); \+ שָמֵָחָה [Est 8:15](https://ref.ly/Esth8:15).

> \[Str4684] †\[מִצְהָלָה] **n.f.** neighing;—pl. cstr. קוֺל מִצְהֲלוֺת אַבִּירָיוֺ [Je 8:16](https://ref.ly/Jer8:16) (‖נַחְרַת סוּסָיוֺ); sf. מִחְהֲלוֺתַיִח [13:27](https://ref.ly/Jer13:27) of idolatrous Judah, under fig. of mare desiring stallion (‖נִאֻפַיִח, זִמַּת זְנוּתֵח).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 12:6; Isaiah 54:1; Isaiah 24:14; Jeremiah 31:7; Esther 8:15; Jeremiah 5:8; Jeremiah 13:27; Jeremiah 8:16; Jeremiah 50:11; Isaiah 10:30


## II צָהַל (id: BDB08033)

\[Str6670] † II. \[צָהַל] **vb. Hiph.** make shining (NH *id.* (rare); ‖ form of צהר, denom. fr. צהרים);—*Inf. cstr.* לְהַצְהִיל פָּנִים מִשָּׁמֶן [Psalm 104:15](https://ref.ly/Ps104:15).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 104:15


## צהר (id: BDB08034)

צהר **(**√of foll.; cf. Ar. ظَهَرَ *appear, mount,* ظَهْرٌ *back,* ظَهِيرَةٌ *midday;* As. *ṣêru* (TelAm. *zu’ru,* etc.), *back;* Aram. טִיהֲרָא, ܛܰܗܪܳܐ MI15 הצהרם, *midday*; LangBN 129; \> Köii. 1:93 (cf. Thes) \= *shine,* ‖זהר, NH Hiph. (rare), for this is in Aram. צהר (rare)**)**; הִצְהִיר [Ecclus 43:3](https://ref.ly/Sir43:3) is denom. from צהרים.

> \[Str6672] † 1\. \[צֹ֫הַר] **\[n.m.]** only pl. צָהֳרַ֫יִם midday, noon, [Dt 28:29](https://ref.ly/Deut28:29)\+, צָהֳרָיִם [Gn 43:16](https://ref.ly/Gen43:16) \+(when sun *mounts* its highest; on form as expanded pl. (not du.) v. Ges§ 88 c and reff.);—usu. **1\.** *noon* as a specif. time of day, [1 K 18:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:29); esp. ´בַּצּ *at noon* [Gn 43:16](https://ref.ly/Gen43:16), [43:25](https://ref.ly/Gen43:25), [Am 8:9](https://ref.ly/Amos8:9), [1 K 18:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:27), [20:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:16), Ct [1:7](https://ref.ly/Song1:7) (resting\-time for flock), [Je 6:4](https://ref.ly/Jer6:4) (opp. evening); ´בְּעֵת צ [Je 20:16](https://ref.ly/Jer20:16) (dist. fr. morning) as time of supposed security [Je 15:8](https://ref.ly/Jer15:8), [Zp 2:4](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:4); also ´מֵהַבֹּקֶר וְעַד הַצּ [1 K 18:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:26), ´עַד־הַצּ [2 K 4:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:20); without ב, as adv., ´צ as time of prayer [Psalm 55:18](https://ref.ly/Ps55:18) (\+בֹּקֶר, עֶרֶב); as time of wasting קֶטֶב [91:6](https://ref.ly/Ps91:6); ´מִשְׁכַּב הַצּ [2 S 4:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:5) *noonday repose.* **2\.** *noon,* as bright, sim. of happiness, blessing, [Is 58:10](https://ref.ly/Isa58:10) (opp. אֲפֵלָה), [Psalm 37:6](https://ref.ly/Ps37:6) (‖כָּאוֺר); cf. [Jb 11:17](https://ref.ly/Job11:17) (Ges § 133 e); v. also ´בְּתוֺח הַצּ [Is 16:3](https://ref.ly/Isa16:3) (opp. חִלֵּח); ´בַּצּ [Dt 28:29](https://ref.ly/Deut28:29), [Is 59:10](https://ref.ly/Isa59:10), [Jb 5:14](https://ref.ly/Job5:14).

> \[Str6672] †II. צֹ֫הַר **n.f.** prob. roof (cf. Ar. As. TelAm. *back;* \> Thes Di al. *light,* window);—תַּעֲשֶה ´צ לַתֵּבָה [Gn 6:16](https://ref.ly/Gen6:16).

> \[Str3323] † I. יִצְהָר **n.\[m.]** fresh oil (newly *appeared,* cf. ‘Ausbruch’ LagBN 129 Köii. 1:93; \> Thes al. that which *shines*);—*abs.* ´יִצ [Ho 2:10](https://ref.ly/Hos2:10) \+, sf. יִצְהָרְֶָ [Dt 7:13](https://ref.ly/Deut7:13) \+;—*fresh oil,* as product of land, in un\-manufactured state, usu. \+ תִּירוֺשׁ, דָּגָן etc., rich possession, gift of, ´י [Ho 2:10](https://ref.ly/Hos2:10), [2:24](https://ref.ly/Hos2:24), [Je 31:12](https://ref.ly/Jer31:12), [Dt 7:13](https://ref.ly/Deut7:13), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Deut11:14), [Jo 2:19](https://ref.ly/Joel2:19), [2:24](https://ref.ly/Joel2:24), so ´חֵלֶב יִצ [Nu 18:12](https://ref.ly/Num18:12); [Dt 28:51](https://ref.ly/Deut28:51), [Hg 1:11](https://ref.ly/Hag1:11), [2 Ch 32:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:28), [Ne 5:11](https://ref.ly/Neh5:11); tithed [Dt 12:17](https://ref.ly/Deut12:17), [14:23](https://ref.ly/Deut14:23); firstfruits for priest [18:4](https://ref.ly/Deut18:4), [Ne 10:38](https://ref.ly/Neh10:38) cf. v:[40](https://ref.ly/Neh10:39), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Neh13:5) (tithe for Levites), v:[12](https://ref.ly/Neh13:12), for priest and Levite [2 Ch 31:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:5); ´זֵית יִצ [2 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:32) *oil\-olive,* i.e. oil\-yielding olive\-trees, cf. ´יִצ alone [Jo 1:10](https://ref.ly/Joel1:10); ´יִצ as *anointing oil* only in ´בְּנֵי הַיִּצ [Zc 4:14](https://ref.ly/Zech4:14), i.e. anointed ones.

> \[Str6671] †\[צָהַר] **vb.denom.** fr. foreg., **Hiph. press** out oil;—*Impf.* 3 mpl. יַצְהִָירוּ [Jb 24:11](https://ref.ly/Job24:11).

> \[Str3324] † II. יִצְהָר **n.pr.m.** Ις(ς)ααρ: Levite, son of Kohath [Ex 6:18](https://ref.ly/Exod6:18), [6:21](https://ref.ly/Exod6:21), [Nu 3:19](https://ref.ly/Num3:19), [16:1](https://ref.ly/Num16:1), [1 Ch 5:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:28), [6:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:3), [6:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:23), [23:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:12), [23:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:18).

> \[Str3325] †יִצְהָרִי **adj.gent.** of foreg., c. art. as subst. coll. ´הַיִּצ [Nu 3:27](https://ref.ly/Num3:27), [1 Ch 24:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:22), [26:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:23), [26:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:29).

> \[Str6673] צַו v. צוה

> חוא (√of foll.; cf. Syr. ܨܐܝ *be foul,* Eth. ጼአ፡; prob. also Ar. وَصيءَ *be polluted;* DlPr 160 Köii. 1, 162 cp. also As. *ṣi', destroy, ruin;* NH צוֺאָה \= BH; Aram. צָאתָא, ܨܐܬܐ, *filth*).

> \[Str6627] †\[צֵאָה] **n.f.** filth, specif. **human excrement**;—sf. צֵאָתְֶָ [Dt 23:14](https://ref.ly/Deut23:14); cstr. בְּגֶלְלֵי צֵאַת הָאָדָם Ez [4:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:12) (as fuel; cf. \[גֵּל] p. 165 supr.).

> \[Str6675,2716] † צֹאָה **n.f.** filth;—abs. ´ח [Is 28:8](https://ref.ly/Isa28:8) (of drunkards’ vomit, cf. קִיא); ´ח fig. of iniquity, cstr. צֹאַת [Is 4:4](https://ref.ly/Isa4:4), sf. צֹאָתוֺ [Pr 30:12](https://ref.ly/Prov30:12); specif. of human *excrement* ( \= \[צֵאָה]) sf. צֵאָתָם [2 K 18:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:27) \= [Is 36:12](https://ref.ly/Isa36:12). (both Qr; Kt יהם(א)חר, cf. \[חֶרֶא] p. 351 supr.).

> \[Str6674] †\[צֹאִי] **adj.** filthy;—pl. צוֺאִים [Zc 3:3](https://ref.ly/Zech3:3), צֹאִים v:[4](https://ref.ly/Zech3:4) (both of garments).

> \[Str6677] צַוָּאר v. I. צור

> \[Str2578,6678] †צוֺבָא, צוֺבָה **n.pr.terr.** Σουβα, rarely Σωβα(λ): an Aramaean kingdom, time of (Saul and) David אֲרַם צוֺבָא [2 S 10:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:6), [10:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:8) (elsewhere צוֺבָה), אֲרַם צוֺבָה [Psalm 60:2](https://ref.ly/Ps60:2) (title); ´הֲדַדְעֶזֶר מֶלֶח צ [2 S 8:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:3), [8:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:5), [8:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:12) \= [1 Ch 18:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:3), [18:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:5), [1 K 11:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:23), [1 Ch 18:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:9); ´צ also [19:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:6); ´חֲמַת צ [2 Ch 8:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:3); orig. home of one of David's heroes [2 S 23:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:36) (perh. rd. also for n.pr.m. מִבְחָר ‖ [1 Ch 11:38](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:38), cf. Dr); ´מַלְכֵי צ [1 S 14:47](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:47) (only here in Saul's time); NöARAM, in Ency. Bib., cf. ZMG xxv (1871\), 113 ff. places between Hamath and Demascus (about *Ḥums,* Emesa), and so DlPa 279 f., who cp. As. city *Ṣubitu,* cf. SchrCOT. [2 S 8:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:8).

## I צוּד (id: BDB08048)

\[Str6679] † I. צוּד **vb.** hunt (NH *id.* (rare), מְצוּדָה \= BH; As. *ṣâdu, hunt,* so Ar. صيد, صَادَ, Aram. צוּד, צִיד, (ܨܘܕ) ܨܳܕ; OAram. צידא *hunting*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. צָד֣וּ [La 4:18](https://ref.ly/Lam4:18); sf. צָדוּנִי [3:52](https://ref.ly/Lam3:52), וְצָדוּם consec. [Je 16:16](https://ref.ly/Jer16:16); *Impf.* 3 ms. יָצוּד [Lv 17:13](https://ref.ly/Lev17:13), etc.; *Imv.* ms. צוּ֫דָה [Gn 27:3](https://ref.ly/Gen27:3); *Inf. abs.* צוֺד [La 3:52](https://ref.ly/Lam3:52); *cstr.* לָצוּד [Gn 27:5](https://ref.ly/Gen27:5); *Pt.* הַצָּד v:[33](https://ref.ly/Gen27:33); —*hunt,* c. acc. 1\. צַיִד [Gn 27:3](https://ref.ly/Gen27:3) Qr (\> Kt צידה), v:[5](https://ref.ly/Gen27:5), v:[33](https://ref.ly/Gen27:33) (all J), צֵיד חַיָּה [Lv 17:13](https://ref.ly/Lev17:13) (H); טֶרֶף [Jb 38:39](https://ref.ly/Job38:39); c. acc. Pers. [Mi 7:2](https://ref.ly/Mic7:2) (\+חֵרֶם, instr.), [Je 16:16](https://ref.ly/Jer16:16) (\+מֵעַל), [Jb 10:16](https://ref.ly/Job10:16), צוֺד צָדוּנִי כַצִּפּוֺד [La 3:52](https://ref.ly/Lam3:52), subj. evil [Psalm 140:12](https://ref.ly/Ps140:12); c. acc. צְעָדֵינוּ [La 4:18](https://ref.ly/Lam4:18), נֶפֶשׁ יְקָרָה [Pr 6:26](https://ref.ly/Prov6:26).—[Ho 9:13](https://ref.ly/Hos9:13) rd. prob. לָצוּד (or לָצַיִד), for MT לְצוֺר, v. צֹר. **Po'l.** *hunt* (keenly, eagerly?), *Impf.* 2 fpl. תְּצוֺדֵדְנָה Ez [13:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:18); *Inf. cstr.* לְצוֺדֵד v:[18](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:18); Pt.fpl. מְצֹדְדוֺת v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:20), v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:20), all c. acc. נְפָשׁוֺת (of magic arts, necromancy, etc.).

BaNB 161\), [Gn 25:28](https://ref.ly/Gen25:28), [27:3](https://ref.ly/Gen27:3) (v. supr.) v:[5](https://ref.ly/Gen27:5), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Gen27:7), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Gen27:19), v:[25](https://ref.ly/Gen27:25), v:[31](https://ref.ly/Gen27:31), v:[33](https://ref.ly/Gen27:33) (all J), cf. ## צָוַח (id: BDB08066)

\[Str6681] †\[צָוַח] **vb.** cry aloud (NH *id.;* so As. *ṣâḫu* (?), Ar. (صيح), صَاحَ, Aram. צְוַח, ܨܘܰܚ; Eth. ፀውዕ፡ *call*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִצְָ\+וחוּ [Is 42:11](https://ref.ly/Isa42:11) (in joy, ‖יָרֹ֫נוּ).

> \[Str6682] †צְוָחָה **n.f.** outcry;—in distress, grief, abs. ´ח [Psalm 144:14](https://ref.ly/Ps144:14); cstr. צִוְחַת [Je 14:2](https://ref.ly/Jer14:2); sf. צִוְחָתֵךְ [46:12](https://ref.ly/Jer46:12); abs. also [Is 24:11](https://ref.ly/Isa24:11) (c. עַל *for, because of*).

> חול (√of foll., cf. *miṣwal* (in Syria), a stone\-lined hollow, or basin, for washing grain, WetzstSiebe ZPV xiv (1891\), 3\).

> \[Str6683] †צוּלָה **n.f.** ocean\-deep;—[Is 44:27](https://ref.ly/Isa44:27).

> \[Str4688] †\[מְצוֺלָה], מְצוּלָה **n.f.** depth, deep;—abs. מְצוּלָה [Jb 41:23](https://ref.ly/Job41:23) \+; pl. abs. מְצוֺלֹת [Ex 15:5](https://ref.ly/Exod15:5), [Ne 9:11](https://ref.ly/Neh9:11), מְצֹלוֺת [Psalm 88:7](https://ref.ly/Ps88:7); cstr. מְצוּלוֺת [Zc 10:11](https://ref.ly/Zech10:11), מְצֻלוֺת [Mi 7:19](https://ref.ly/Mic7:19), [Psalm 68:23](https://ref.ly/Ps68:23); —*depth,* pl. יָם ´מ *depths of the sea* [Mi 7:19](https://ref.ly/Mic7:19), [Psalm 68:23](https://ref.ly/Ps68:23); abs. [Ex 15:5](https://ref.ly/Exod15:5) (of Red Sea) [Ne 9:11](https://ref.ly/Neh9:11) (*id.*); fig. of deep distress [Psalm 88:7](https://ref.ly/Ps88:7) (‖בּוֺר תַּחְתִיּוֺת); יְאֹר ´מ [Zc 10:11](https://ref.ly/Zech10:11) (i.e. of Nile); sg. \= *the deep sea, deep,* [Psalm 107:24](https://ref.ly/Ps107:24), [Jon 2:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:4), [Jb 41:23](https://ref.ly/Job41:23) (in hyperb. descript. of crocodile); *depth* of marsh, swamp, ´בִּיוֵן מ [Psalm 69:3](https://ref.ly/Ps69:3) *in mire of (the) depth,* fig. of distress (‖מַעֲמַקֵּי־מַיִם), cf. ´מ alone v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ps69:16) (‖בְּאֵר).

> \[Str4699] †מְצֻלָה **n.f.** dub. word: [Zc 1:8](https://ref.ly/Zech1:8) the myrtles which are ´בַּמּ, appar. some locality about Jerus., called *the basin, hollow;* GASm *glen* or *valley\-bottom;* poss. is מְצֻלָּה, \< מְצִלָּה, *shadow* (III. צלל).

## צוּם (id: BDB08072)

\[Str6684] †\[צוּם] **vb.** abstain from food, fast (NH *id.;* Ar. (صوم) صَامَ, Eth. ፆመ Aram. צוּם, ܨܳܡ);—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms. צַמְתָּ [2 S 12:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:21); 2 mpl. צַמְתֶּם [Zc 7:5](https://ref.ly/Zech7:5), sf. צַמְתֻּנִי v:[5](https://ref.ly/Zech7:5); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיָּ֫צָם [2 S 12:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:16), וַיָּצוָֺם [1 K 21:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:27); 1 s. אָצוּם[Est 4:16](https://ref.ly/Esth4:16), etc.; *Imv.* mpl. צוּמוּ v:[16](https://ref.ly/Esth4:16); *Inf. abs.* צוֺם [Zc 7:5](https://ref.ly/Zech7:5); *Pt.* צָם [2 S 12:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:23), [Ne 1:4](https://ref.ly/Neh1:4); —*fast,* in mourning the dead [1 S 31:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:13) \= [1 Ch 10:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:12), [2 S 1:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:12); in worship (contrition, intercession, etc.) [Jn 20:26](https://ref.ly/John20:26), [1 S 7:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:6), [2 S 12:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:16) (c. acc. cogn. צוֺם; ‖בִּקֵּשׁ), v:[23](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:23) and (\+בָּכָה), v:[21](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:21), v:[22](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:22) (q.v. for explanation), [Je 14:12](https://ref.ly/Jer14:12), [Zc 7:5](https://ref.ly/Zech7:5) (\+סְפוֺד), הֲצוֺם צַמְתֻּנִי v:[5](https://ref.ly/Zech7:5) (Ges§ 117 x) *was it at all unto me* \[´י] *that ye fasted ?* וָאֱהִי צָם [Ne 1:4](https://ref.ly/Neh1:4) (periphr. conj.; \+מִתְפַּלֵּל), cf. ## צוּף (id: BDB08076)

\[Str6687] †\[צוּף] **vb.** flow, overflow, \[float] (NH *id.;* so Aram. טוּף, ܛܳܦ;—Ar. طَوْفٌ *skin\-raft* is loan\-wd. Frä220);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. צָפוּ־מַיִם עַל־ראֹשִׁי [La 3:54](https://ref.ly/Lam3:54) (fig.). **Hiph. 1\.** *Pf.* *cause to flow over,* הֵצִיף אֶת־מֵי יַם־סוּף עַל־פְּנֵיהֶם [Dt 11:4](https://ref.ly/Deut11:4). **2\.** *cause to float: Impf.* 3 ms. וַיָּצֶף הַבַּרְזֶל [2 K 6:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:6).

> \[Str6688] † I. צוּף **n.m.** (honey\-) comb (as *exuding* honey);—only fig.: cstr. צוּף־דְּבַשׁ [Pr 16:24](https://ref.ly/Prov16:24); pl. נֹפֶת צוּפִים [Psalm 19:11](https://ref.ly/Ps19:11) (\+דְּבַשׁ), LXX κηρίον, κηρία, Vulgate *favus*.

> \[Str6689] † II. צוּף **n.m.** Ephr. ancestor of Elḳanah and Samuel, ´בֶּן־ח [1 S 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:1) (ἐν Νασειβ, LXXL υἱοῦ Σωφ), [1 Ch 6:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:20) Qr LXX Vulgate (Kt ציף); appar. \= צוֺפַי v:[11](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:11) (Levite), Σουφ(ει): hence prob. אֶרֶץ צוּף [1 S 9:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:5) (in Benj., τὴν (γὴν)Σ(ε)ιφ(α)).

> \[Str6689,7436] †צוּפִי **adj.gent.** Ṣuphite;—so read prob. for צוֺפִים [1 S 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:1) (We Klo Dr and most mod., after LXX Σ(ε)ιφα, A Σωφιμ).

> \[Str6824] †\[צָפָה] **n.f.** out\-flow;—sf. צָפָֽתְ Ez [32:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:6) *thine outflow* (flowing blood), Co Bthl Toy Krae.

> \[Str6689,7436] צוֺפַי \= II. צוּף. צוֺפִים v. צוּפִי. above

Köii. l, 60\);—**1\.** *blossom, flower,* [Nu 17:23](https://ref.ly/Num17:23) (P; of Aaron's rod); (fig. of man [Is 40:7](https://ref.ly/Isa40:7), [40:8](https://ref.ly/Isa40:8) (both ´נָבֵל צ; ‖חָצִיר), cf. sim. צִיץ הַשָּדֶה v:[6](https://ref.ly/Isa40:6) (‖ *id.*), [Psalm 103:15](https://ref.ly/Ps103:15), and ´צ alone [Jb 14:2](https://ref.ly/Job14:2); נֹבֵל ´צ [Is 28:1](https://ref.ly/Isa28:1) (fig. of Samaria), so prob. also v:[4](https://ref.ly/Isa28:4) (for MT ´צִיצַת נ; so Marti); פְּטוּרֵי צִצִּים, as ornaments of temple [1 K 6:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:18), [6:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:29), [6:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:32), [6:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:35). **2\.** *shining thing,* plate of gold, constituting the diadem on front of high priest's mitre, [Lv 8:9](https://ref.ly/Lev8:9), [Ex 28:36](https://ref.ly/Exod28:36), [39:30](https://ref.ly/Exod39:30) (all P).—I, III. צִיץ, v. p. 851 infr.

## צוּק (id: BDB08092)

\[Str6694] †II \[צוּק] **vb.** pour out, melt (‖יצק);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. (acc. to Thes al.) צָקוּן [Is 26:16](https://ref.ly/Isa26:16), but v. infr.; *Impf.* 3 ms. וְאֶבֶן יָצוּק נְחוּשָׁה [Jb 28:2](https://ref.ly/Job28:2) (rd. perh. יָצוּקוּ, v. Bu) *and stone* (ore, *men*) *melt it into copper;* צוּר יָצוּק עִמָּדִי פַּלְגֵי־שָׁמֶן [Jb 29:6](https://ref.ly/Job29:6) *the rock used to pour out beside me streams of oil.*—[Is 26:16](https://ref.ly/Isa26:16) (as above) must mean *they poured out* (uttered) a whisper (לַחַשׁ; i. e. prayer); but form most improb.; Koppe Gr Di Du al. prop. צְקוֺן לַחַשׁ (from assumed צָקוֺן, I. צוק) \= *constraint of* (like that of) *magic;* \< Houb Kit צָֽעֲקוּ בְלַחַץ ´וגו, or CheHpt צָעַקְנוּ מִלַּחַץ כִּי מוּסָֽרְ לָנוּ.

[Psalm 75:6](https://ref.ly/Ps75:6) speak not with *arrogant neck* (si Vera l.; but rd. perh. בַּצּוּר *against the Rock,* LXX. κατὰ τοῦ θεοῦ, Bae Hup\-Now Che Kau); of wicked, rushing against God [Jb 15:26](https://ref.ly/Job15:26).—עַל־צַוָּארֵנוּ נִרְדָּ֔פְנוּ [La 5:5](https://ref.ly/Lam5:5) is dubious; Matthes Löhr Bae ´עֹל עַל־וגו; Bu ´עֹל צַוּ, and הֲדָפָנוּ or יֶהְדְּפֵנוּ for ´נִר. **2\.** *neck* of animals, camels (wearing ornaments) Ju [8:21](https://ref.ly/Judg8:21), [8:26](https://ref.ly/Judg8:26); heifer [Ho 10:11](https://ref.ly/Hos10:11) (i. e. place of yoke, in fig. of Ephr.); horse [Jb 39:19](https://ref.ly/Job39:19), crocodile [41:14](https://ref.ly/Job41:14).

## III צוּר (id: BDB08100)

\[Str6696] †III. \[צוּר] **vb.** shew hostility to, treat as foe (c. acc. Pers.) (akin to II. צרר, to which SS assign the forms, but cf. Ar. ضَارَ *act unjustly,* also *defraud;* Syr. ܥܝܳܪܬܳܐ, *rival wife*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 s. וְצַרְתִּ֫י אֶת־צֹרֲרֶיְ [Ex 23:22](https://ref.ly/Exod23:22) (subj. ´י; ‖וְאָיַבְתִּ֫י אֶת־אֹיְבֶיְ); *Impf.* 2 ms. אַל־תָּ֫צַר אֶת־מוֺאָב [Dt 2:9](https://ref.ly/Deut2:9); 2 mpl. sf. אַל־תְּצֻרֵם v:[19](https://ref.ly/Deut2:19); *Pt.* הַצָּרִים אוֺתָם [Est 8:11](https://ref.ly/Esth8:11).

* **Associated Passages:** Deuteronomy 2:19; Esther 8:11; Exodus 23:22; Deuteronomy 2:9

## צָחַח (id: BDB08113)

\[Str6705] †\[צָחַח] **vb.** be dazzling (Targum צַחְצַח *polish;* Syr. ܨܰܚ *be scorched,* Aph. *declare in writing* (make clear), ܨܰܚܺܝܚܳܐ *clarus, lucidus, fulgidus;* صَحْصَحَانٌ, صَحْصَحٌ, *smooth, plain*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl צַחוּ מֵהָּלָב [La 4:7](https://ref.ly/Lam4:7) (‖זַכּוּ מִשֶּׁלֶג), *they are more dazzling* (i.e. *whiter*) *than milk* (of effeminate skin).

> \[Str6703] †צַח **adj.** dazzling, glowing, clear;—חֹם צַח [Is 18:4](https://ref.ly/Isa18:4) *glowing heat;* רוּחַ צַח [Je 4:11](https://ref.ly/Jer4:11) *glowing wind;* דּוֺדִי צַח וְאָדוֺם Ct [5:10](https://ref.ly/Song5:10) *my beloved is dazzling (white) and ruddy* (cf. [La 4:7](https://ref.ly/Lam4:7)); fpl. לְדַבֵּר צַחוֺת [Is 32:4](https://ref.ly/Isa32:4) *to speak clear* (words), *clearly*.

> \[Str6706] †\[צָחִיחַ] **n.\[m.]** shining, glaring, surface cstr. עְחִיחַ סֶלַע i.e. a smooth, bare, rock Ez [24:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:7), [24:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:8), [26:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:4), [26:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:14); pl. בַּצְּחִיחִים [Ne 4:7](https://ref.ly/Neh4:7) Qr (Kt בַּצְּחִחִיִּים), *in glaring, bare places (?)*.

> \[Str6707] †צְחִיחָה **n.f.** scorched land;—´ח [Psalm 68:7](https://ref.ly/Ps68:7).

> \[Str6710] †\[צַחְצָחָה] **n.\[f.]** scorched region;—pl. צַחְצָחוֺת [Is 58:11](https://ref.ly/Isa58:11).

> חצן (√ of foll.; NH צַחֲנָה \= BH; Aram. צְחַנְתָּא *stinking fluid*, ܨܚܢ, ܨܚܢܐ *foul*).

> \[Str6709] †\[צַחֲנָה] **n.f.** stench;—sf. צַחֲנָתוֺ [Jo 2:20](https://ref.ly/Joel2:20) (‖בָּאְשׁוֺ ).—cf. עפר צחנה [Ecclus 11:12](https://ref.ly/Sir11:12).

## II ציר (id: BDB08148)

II. ציר (√ of foll.; ? *turn\< revolve;* NH צִיר *pivot* (of door), *hinge* so Ar. صَايءِرٌ (cf. سَيَّار, Dozyi. 712 b); perh. As. *ṣirru;* Aram. ܨܰܝܳܪܬܳܐ צִירְתָּא, صَلَْء).

852:a. II. ציר: cf. Ar. صار (med. *a*) \= *turn* or *incline* one’s face or neck, or a bough, etc. Lane1744.

## I צָלַל (id: BDB08161)

\[Str6750] † I. \[צָלַל] **vb.** tingle, quiver (NH צִלְצֵל *whirr* (rare); Ar. ܨܰܠ; Targum צְלַל; Syr. ܨܪܨܪ);—*Pf.* 3 pl. צָֽלְלוּ Hb [3:16](https://ref.ly/Hab3:16); *Impf.* 3 fpl. תְּצִלֶּינָה [1 S 3:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:11), תִּצַּלְנָה (Ges§ 67 g) [2 K 21:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:12), [Je 19:3](https://ref.ly/Jer19:3); —*tingle,* of ears, at horrid sound [1 S 3:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:11), [2 K 21:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:12), [Je 19:3](https://ref.ly/Jer19:3) (all of dreadful news); of lips, *quiver* in terror Hb [3:16](https://ref.ly/Hab3:16).

> \[Str6767] I. \[צִלְצָל] **n.\[m.]** whirring, buzzing;—cstr. אֶרֶץ צִלְצַל כְּנָפָיִם [Is 18:1](https://ref.ly/Isa18:1) *land of buzzing of* (insects ’) *wings* (so prob.; other views v. in Di).

> \[Str6767] † II. \[צִלְצָל] **n.\[m.]** spear (fr. *whizzing*);—cstr. צִלְצַל דָּגִים [Jb 40:31](https://ref.ly/Job40:31) *fish\-spear, harpoon.*

> \[Str6767] † צְלָצַָל **n.m.** a whirring locust (cf. As. *ṣarṣaru,* Ar. ܨܺܝܨܪܳܐ Syr. ܨܰܪܨܽܘܪܳܐ; Lag: Ges. Abh. 145, N. 9\);—[Dt 28:42](https://ref.ly/Deut28:42) (devouring; not identif.; TrNHB 313\).

> \[Str6767] † צֶלְצְלִים **n.m.pl.** musical instr. of percussion, cymbals (with *clashing* sound; NH צֶלְצַל, Aram. צֶלְצְלִין, ܨܺܝܨܠܴܐ) ;—´ח [2 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:5) (\+ מְנַעַנְעִים, q. v.; ‖ [1 Ch 13:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:8) מְצִלְתַּיִם); cstr. צִלְצְלֵי־שָׁמַע [Psalm 150:5](https://ref.ly/Ps150:5), תְּרוּעָה v:[5](https://ref.ly/Ps150:5); —cf. NowArch. i. 272 f. 272 f. BenzArch. 277 WePsalm Psalm Eng. Tr. 232\.

> \[Str4698] † \[מְצִלָּה] **n.f.** bell (fr.*tinkling*);—pl. cstr. מְצִלּוֺת הַסּוּס [Zc 14:20](https://ref.ly/Zech14:20).

> \[Str4700] † מְצִלְתַּיִם **n.f.du.** cymbals (appar. later equiv. of צֶלְצְלִים where v. reff.);—´מ [1 Ch 13:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:8) (‖ [2 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:5) v. צֶלְצְלִים), [15:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:19), [16:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:5), [16:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:42), [25:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:6), [2 Ch 5:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:12), [5:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:13), [29:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:25), [Ezr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:10), [Ne 12:27](https://ref.ly/Neh12:27); תָּיִם\- [1 Ch 15:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:16), [15:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:28), [25:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:1).

## II צָלַל (id: BDB08168)

LXX. Co Toy Krae).

[Ho 14:8](https://ref.ly/Hos14:8), Ez [17:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:23), [31:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:6), [31:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:12), [31:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:17), [Psalm 80:11](https://ref.ly/Ps80:11), Ct [2:3](https://ref.ly/Song2:3) (of refreshment, delight); of rock [Is 32:2](https://ref.ly/Isa32:2) (sim.); קֹרָתִי ´ח [Gn 19:8](https://ref.ly/Gen19:8) *shadow of my roof;* of cloud [Is 25:4](https://ref.ly/Isa25:4) and (fig. of ´י's protection) [4:6](https://ref.ly/Isa4:6), [25:4](https://ref.ly/Isa25:4), so prob. > \[Str6753] † הַצְּלֶלְפּוֺנִי **n.pr.f.** in Judah (*give shade,* thou *that turnest to me!*) [1 Ch 4:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:3), Εσηλεββων, etc.

> IV. חלל (√ of foll.; meaning dub.; Eth. መጸለት፡ is *unleavened bread* (Di: 1257 as *pure,* unfermented, cf. ܨܠ sub II. חלל), more prob. would be designation of shape).

> \[Str6742] † צלול Kt, צְלִיל Qr **n.m.** cake, round loaf;—שְעֹרִיס Ju [7:13](https://ref.ly/Judg7:13) (cf. GFM).

> חלם (√ of foll.; NöZMG xi (1886\), 733 f, cp. Ar. صَلَمَ, *cut off* (e.g. an ear, anose);—NH חֶלֶם \= BH, so As. *ṣalmu,* Sab. חלם Gildemeister: ZMG xxiv (1870\), 180 CISiv, no. 2, 1\. 4, Aram. חַלְמָא, ܨܠܡܐ, OAram. חלמא, so Nab. Palm. (also צלמתא of woman's statue)).

> \[Str6754] † צֶ֫לֶם **n.m.**: Ez [16:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:17) image (something *cut out,* cf. פֶּסֶל; Nö ‘Schnitzbild’);—´ח abs. [Psalm 39:7](https://ref.ly/Ps39:7), cstr. [Gn 1:27](https://ref.ly/Gen1:27) \+; sf. צַלְמוֺ v:[27](https://ref.ly/Gen1:27), [5:3](https://ref.ly/Gen5:3), צַלְמֵנוּ [1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26), צַלְמָם [Psalm 73:20](https://ref.ly/Ps73:20); pl. cstr. צַלְמֵי [1 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:5), [6:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:5) \+, sf. צְלָמָיו [2 K 11:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:18), [2 Ch 23:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:17), צַלְמֵיכֶם [Am 5:26](https://ref.ly/Amos5:26); —**1\.** *images* of tumours and mice (of gold) [1 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:5), [6:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:5), [6:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:11); esp. of heathen gods [Am 5:26](https://ref.ly/Amos5:26) (text dub.; del. We as gloss, cf. GASm Dr), [2 K 11:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:18) \= [2 Ch 23:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:17) (both c. vb. שִׁבְּרוּ), Ez [7:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:20), so זָכָר ´ח [16:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:17) (i.e. in male form, acc. to fig. of harlotry for idolatry); מַסֵכֹתָם [Nu 33:52](https://ref.ly/Num33:52) *their molten images;* of painted pictures of men Ez [23:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:14). **2\.** *image, likeness,* of resemblance, ´בְּ (בָּרָא) עָשָה, of God's making man in his own image, [Gn 1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26)(‖כִּדְמוּתֵנוּ), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Gen1:27), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Gen1:27), [9:6](https://ref.ly/Gen9:6), ´כְּ [5:3](https://ref.ly/Gen5:3) (‖בִּדְמוּתוֺ; all P). **3\.** fig. \= *mere, empty, image, semblance,* ´בְּח [Psalm 39:7](https://ref.ly/Ps39:7) *as* (ב essentiae) *a* (mere) *semblance man walks about;* צַלְמָם תִּבְזֶה [73:20](https://ref.ly/Ps73:20) *thou wilt despise their semblance*.

## צלע (id: BDB08185)

I . צלע (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. ضَلَعَ, *decline, deviate,* ضَلِعٌ *curved;* NH צֵלָע \= BH, so Ar. ضِلَعٌ As. *ṣêlu,* BAram. עֲלַע, עילְעָא, Syr. ܐܶܠܥܳܐ).

> \[Str6763] צֵלָע:41 **n.f.** (**m.** [1 K 6:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:34), and appar. [Ex 26:26](https://ref.ly/Exod26:26)) rib, side;—abs. ´ח [Gn 2:22](https://ref.ly/Gen2:22) \+, cstr. צֶ֫לַע [Ex 26:26](https://ref.ly/Exod26:26) \+, also צֵ֫לַע [2 S 16:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:13); sf. צַלְעוֺ [Ex 25:12](https://ref.ly/Exod25:12) \+; pl. צְלָעִים [1 K 6:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:34), צְלָעוֺת v:[3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:3) \+; cstr. צַלְעֹת Ez [41:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:26) \+; sf. צַלְעֹתָיו [Gn 2:21](https://ref.ly/Gen2:21) \+; —**1\.** *rib* of man [Gn 2:21](https://ref.ly/Gen2:21), [2:22](https://ref.ly/Gen2:22) (J). **2\.** *rib* of hill, i.e. ridge, or terrace [2 S 16:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:13). **3\.** *side\-chambers or cells* (enclosing temple like ribs) [1 K 6:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:5), [6:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:6) (rd. ´הַצּ for היצוע, v. \[יָצִיעַ]), [7:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:3), so of Ezek.'s temple Ez [41:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:5) \+ 10 t. 41 (on text v. Co Toy Krae). **4\.** *ribs* of cedar and fir, i.e. *planks, boards* (pl.), of temple wall [1 K 6:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:15), [6:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:16) floor v:[15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:15). **5\.** *leaves* of door v:[34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:34). **6\.** (in P) *side,* of ark (אֶרוֺן) [Ex 25:12](https://ref.ly/Exod25:12), [25:12](https://ref.ly/Exod25:12), [25:14](https://ref.ly/Exod25:14) \= [37:3](https://ref.ly/Exod37:3), [37:3](https://ref.ly/Exod37:3), [37:5](https://ref.ly/Exod37:5); of tabern. (מִשְׁכָּן) [26:20](https://ref.ly/Exod26:20) (‖פֵּאָה v:[18](https://ref.ly/Exod26:18)), v:[26](https://ref.ly/Exod26:26), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Exod26:27), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Exod26:27) \= [36:25](https://ref.ly/Exod36:25) (‖ ´פ v:[23](https://ref.ly/Exod36:23)), v:[31](https://ref.ly/Exod36:31), v:[32](https://ref.ly/Exod36:32), [26:35](https://ref.ly/Exod26:35), [26:35](https://ref.ly/Exod26:35); of altar [27:7](https://ref.ly/Exod27:7) \= [38:7](https://ref.ly/Exod38:7), [30:4](https://ref.ly/Exod30:4) \= [37:27](https://ref.ly/Exod37:27).—[Je 20:10](https://ref.ly/Jer20:10), [Jb 18:12](https://ref.ly/Job18:12) v. צֶלַע sub II. צלע.

> \[Str6762] † צֵלַע **n.pr.loc.** in Benj. [Jos 18:28](https://ref.ly/Josh18:28), A LXXL Σελα \[(ε) λαφ]; burial\-place of Saul [2 S 21:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:14) (צֵלָֹע), ἐν τῇ πλευρᾷ; poss. \=TelAm. *Zilu,* so ZimZA vi. 258 JastrJBL xi (1892\), 105 (Wkl, however, thinks of סלע).

## II צָלַע (id: BDB08188)

\[Str6760] † II. \[צָלַע] **vb.** limp (Ar. ظَلَعَ , Aram. טלע Aph.);—**Qal** *Pt.* צֹלֵעַ עַל־יְרֵכוֺ [Gn 32:32](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32) (J; of Jacob); f. הַצֹלֵעָה as subst., of personif. Judah as flock \[צאֹן] [Mi 4:6](https://ref.ly/Mic4:6), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Mic4:7), [Zp 3:19](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:19).

> \[Str6761] † צֶ֫לַע **n.** \[**m.**] limping, stumbling;—fig. of calamity, abs. ´לְח [Psalm 38:18](https://ref.ly/Ps38:18)  *I am ready* (נָכוֺן) *for stumbling* (and falling); sf. בְּצַלְעִי [Psalm 35:15](https://ref.ly/Ps35:15) at *my stumbliing;* ´שֹׁמְרֵי צ [Je 20:10](https://ref.ly/Jer20:10) *watchers of my stumbling;* אֵיד נָכוֺן לְצַלְעוֺ [Jb 18:12](https://ref.ly/Job18:12) *ruin is ready for his stumbling.*\-Vid. also צֵלָע cstr.

> \[Str6764] † צָלָף **n.pr.m.** (NH *caper\-plant*);—father of a wall\-builder [Ne 3:30](https://ref.ly/Neh3:30), Σελε (φ), etc.

> \[Str6765] † צְלָפְחָד **n.pr.m.** of Manasseh, [Nu 26:33](https://ref.ly/Num26:33), [26:23](https://ref.ly/Num26:23), [27:1](https://ref.ly/Num27:1), [27:7](https://ref.ly/Num27:7), [36:2](https://ref.ly/Num36:2), [36:6](https://ref.ly/Num36:6), [36:10](https://ref.ly/Num36:10), [36:11](https://ref.ly/Num36:11), [Jos 17:3](https://ref.ly/Josh17:3), [1 Ch 7:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:15), [7:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:15), Σαλπααδ, etc.

> \[Str6766] † צֶלְצַח **n.pr.loc.** in Benj. [1 S 10:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:2) (si vera 1\.; prob. crpt. (Dr); HPS conj. מִצֵּלַע, cf. [2 S 21:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:14)).

> \[Str6767] † I, II. \[ צִלְצָל], צְלָצַָל, צֶלְצְלִיםv. 1\. חלל. P. 852

> \[Str6768] † צֶ֫לֶק **n.pr.m.** Ammonite hero of David [2 S 23:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:37) \= [1 Ch 11:39](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:39), Ελειε, Σελ (λ) η (κ), Σαλααδ, etc.

> \[Str6769] צִלְּתַי v. III. חלל. P.853

## צָמֵא (id: BDB08196)

\[Str6770] † \[צָמֵא] vb. be thirsty (NH \= BH (rare); so Ar. ظَمِىَٔ Eth. ጸምአ፡: As. *ṣûmu, thirst,* etc.);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. צָֽמְאָה [Psalm 42:3](https://ref.ly/Ps42:3), [63:2](https://ref.ly/Ps63:2); 2 fs. וְצָמִת consec. [Ru 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:9) (Ges§ 75 qq); 1 s. צָמֵתִי Ju [4:19](https://ref.ly/Judg4:19) (Ges§ 74 k); 3 pl. צָֽמְאוּ [Is 28:21](https://ref.ly/Isa28:21); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִּצְמָא [Ex 17:3](https://ref.ly/Exod17:3), Ju [15:18](https://ref.ly/Judg15:18); 3 mpl. יִצְמָאוּ [Is 49:10](https://ref.ly/Isa49:10), ´וַיִּצ Jb 24:1l; 2 mpl. תִּצְמָאוּ [Is 65:13](https://ref.ly/Isa65:13); —*be thirsty* Ju [4:19](https://ref.ly/Judg4:19), [15:18](https://ref.ly/Judg15:18), [Ru 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:9), [Is 48:21](https://ref.ly/Isa48:21), [49:10](https://ref.ly/Isa49:10) (‖יִרְעָבוּ), [65:13](https://ref.ly/Isa65:13) (‖תִּרְעָבוּ) [Jb 24:11](https://ref.ly/Job24:11); c. ל rei, [Ex 17:3](https://ref.ly/Exod17:3) (E); fig. צָֽמְאָה נַפְשִׁי לֵאלֹהִים. [Psalm 42:3](https://ref.ly/Ps42:3) *my soul thirsteth for God,* cf. [63:2](https://ref.ly/Ps63:2).

> \[Str6772] † צָמָא **n.** \[**m.**] thirst;—abs. ´ח [Ex 17:3](https://ref.ly/Exod17:3) \+, sf. צְמָאִי Psalm [69:22](https://ref.ly/Ps69:22), etc.;—[Ex 17:3](https://ref.ly/Exod17:3) (E), Ju [15:18](https://ref.ly/Judg15:18), [Ho 2:5](https://ref.ly/Hos2:5), [Am 8:13](https://ref.ly/Amos8:13), [La 4:4](https://ref.ly/Lam4:4), [Ne 9:20](https://ref.ly/Neh9:20), [Psalm 69:22](https://ref.ly/Ps69:22); ‖רָעָב [Is 5:13](https://ref.ly/Isa5:13) (vand. H. צָמָה), [Dt 28:48](https://ref.ly/Deut28:48), [Ne 9:15](https://ref.ly/Neh9:15), [2 Ch 32:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:11); of wild asses [Psalm 104:11](https://ref.ly/Ps104:11), of fish ´בַּצּ [Is 50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2), but rd. perh. (or בַּצְּמֵאָה) וּבְהֶמְתָּם בַּצָּמֵא Gunkschöpf. 98 CheHpt Marti (cf. [Is 44:3](https://ref.ly/Isa44:3) sub foll.); fig. of grief of exile [Is 41:17](https://ref.ly/Isa41:17), cf. אֶרֶץ צִיָּה וְצָמָא Ez [19:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:13); c. ל, *thirst for* water [Am 8:11](https://ref.ly/Amos8:11).—´בַּצּ [Je 48:18](https://ref.ly/Jer48:18) is prob. corrupt; read perhaps לָאָרֶץ (Gie), or בֶּעָפָר.

> \[Str6771] † צָמֵא **adj.** thirsty;—abs. ´ח [Is 21:14](https://ref.ly/Isa21:14) \+; fs. צְמֵאָה [Dt 29:18](https://ref.ly/Deut29:18); pl. צְמֵאִים [Psalm 107:5](https://ref.ly/Ps107:5); —*thirsty,* usu. \+ (or ‖) רָעֵב: 2 S [17:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:29), [Pr 25:21](https://ref.ly/Prov25:21), [Psalm 107:5](https://ref.ly/Ps107:5), as subst. *thirsty one* [Is 29:8](https://ref.ly/Isa29:8), [32:6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6); so also [21:14](https://ref.ly/Isa21:14), and (fig. of thirst for ´י's favour) [55:1](https://ref.ly/Isa55:1); צָמֵא [44:3](https://ref.ly/Isa44:3) \= *thirsty land* (Gunk1.c. prop. צְמֵאָה); אֶת־הַצְּמֵאָה [Dt 29:18](https://ref.ly/Deut29:18) *the watered* (plant, herbage) *with the thirsty,* fig. of *entire* people.

> \[Str6773] † צִמְאָה **n.f.** parched condition;—[Je 2:25](https://ref.ly/Jer2:25) spare thythroat ´מִח *from being parched* (in fig.).

> \[Str6774] † צִמָּאוֺן **n.** \[**m.]** thirsty ground;—abs. ´ח [Dt 8:15](https://ref.ly/Deut8:15), [Is 35:7](https://ref.ly/Isa35:7), [Psalm 107:33](https://ref.ly/Ps107:33).

## צָמַד (id: BDB08201)

\[Str6775] † \[צָמַד] **vb.** bind, join (Ar. ضَمَدَ *bind, wind,* specif. of girl with two lovers WeGGN 1893, 470; *fasten* (cattle) to yoke Dozy, مِضْمَد *yoke,* *Id.;* As.*ṣmâdu, bind, harness,* so Eth. ፀመደ፡ and III. *be attached, attach oneself,* specif. *be* (religiously) *devoted;* Aram. צְמַד, ܨܡܰܕ, *bind*);—**Niph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִּצָּ֫מֶד [Nu 25:3](https://ref.ly/Num25:3); 3 mpl. וַיִּצָּֽדְֶוּ [Psalm 106:28](https://ref.ly/Ps106:28); *Pt.* pl. נִעצמָדִים [Nu 25:5](https://ref.ly/Num25:5); *join, attach, oneself* to (ל) Baa1 Peor, i.e. adopt his worship [Nu 25:3](https://ref.ly/Num25:3), [25:5](https://ref.ly/Num25:5) (JE), whence [Psalm 106:28](https://ref.ly/Ps106:28). **Pu.** *Pt.* f. צְצֻמֶּ֫דֶת [2 S 20:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:8) a sword *bound* upon (עַל) his loins. **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 fs. תַּצְמִיד מִרְמָה [Psalm 50:19](https://ref.ly/Ps50:19) *thy tongue combineth* (fitteth together, frameth) *deceit* (\> denom. fr. צֶמֶד Gerber: 170\).

## צָמַח (id: BDB08206)

\[Str6779] † צָמַח **vb.** sprout, spring up (NH *id.,* so Targum צְמַח; Sir. ܨܡܰܚ *spring or shine forth,* ܨܶܡܚܳܐ *shining,* also *sprout;* Ph. צמח *posterity*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. צָֽמַח־בּוֺ [Lv 13:37](https://ref.ly/Lev13:37); 3 pl. וְצָֽמְחוּ consec. [Is 44:4](https://ref.ly/Isa44:4); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִצְמַח [Jb 5:6](https://ref.ly/Job5:6), 3 fpl. תִּצְמַחְנָה [Is 42:9](https://ref.ly/Isa42:9), etc.; *Pt.* צֹמֵחַ [Ex 10:5](https://ref.ly/Exod10:5), צוֺמֵחַ [Ec 2:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:6); fpl. צֹמְחוֺת [Gn 41:6](https://ref.ly/Gen41:6), [41:23](https://ref.ly/Gen41:23); —*sprout, spring up:* **1\.** of plants, trees, etc. [Gn 2:5](https://ref.ly/Gen2:5), [Ex 10:5](https://ref.ly/Exod10:5) (both J), [Gn 41:6](https://ref.ly/Gen41:6), [41:23](https://ref.ly/Gen41:23) (E), Ez [17:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:6) (in fig.); once (late) יַעַר צוֺמֵחַ עֵצִים [Ec 2:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:6) *wood sprouting with trees* (full of growing trees); fig. of future ruler (v. צֶמַח ) [Zc 6:12](https://ref.ly/Zech6:12), posterity [Is 44:4](https://ref.ly/Isa44:4), cf. [Jb 8:19](https://ref.ly/Job8:19), trouble(עָמָל) [Is 5:6](https://ref.ly/Isa5:6), truth (אֱמֶת) [Psalm 85:12](https://ref.ly/Ps85:12), restoration (אֲרֻכָֽתְ) [Is 58:8](https://ref.ly/Isa58:8), future events [Is 42:9](https://ref.ly/Isa42:9). **2\.** of hair [Lv 13:37](https://ref.ly/Lev13:37) (P; cf. **Pi.**). **Pi.** *grow abunduntly,* always of hair;—Pf. 3 ms. צִמֵּחַ Ez [16:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:7) (in fig.): *Impf.* 3 ms. יִצַמַּח [2 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:5) \= [1 Ch 19:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:5) (of beard); *Inf. cstr.* לְצַמֵּחַ Ju [16:22](https://ref.ly/Judg16:22) (Samson's hair). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. וְהִצְמִיחָהּ [Is 55:10](https://ref.ly/Isa55:10) *Impf.* 3 ms. יַצְמִיחַ [2 S 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:5), [Is 61:11](https://ref.ly/Isa61:11), וַיַּצְמַח [Gn 2:9](https://ref.ly/Gen2:9), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* לְהַצְמִיחַ [Jb 38:27](https://ref.ly/Job38:27); *Pt.* מַצְמִיחַ [Psalm 104:14](https://ref.ly/Ps104:14), [147:8](https://ref.ly/Ps147:8); —**1\.** *cause to grow,* c. acc. of plant, subj. ´י [Gn 2:9](https://ref.ly/Gen2:9), [Psalm 104:14](https://ref.ly/Ps104:14), cf. [Jb 38:27](https://ref.ly/Job38:27), c. 2 acc. [Psalm 147:8](https://ref.ly/Ps147:8) *who causeth mts. to sprout grass;* fig., obj. צֶמַח צְדָקָה [Je 33:15](https://ref.ly/Jer33:15); *horn* (קֶרֶן) of Isr. Ez [29:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:21), of David [Psalm 132:17](https://ref.ly/Ps132:17); righteousness, etc. Is 61:llb; cf. also [2 S 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:5) (if interrog., v. Dr Bu ; otherwise HPS); subj. ground [Gn 3:18](https://ref.ly/Gen3:18) cf. [Is 61:11a](https://ref.ly/Isa61:11) (sim.), [Dt 29:22](https://ref.ly/Deut29:22) (obj. om.); also (fig.) [Is 45:8](https://ref.ly/Isa45:8) (cf. Di Kit). **2\.** of rain, *cause* the earth *to sprout* (abs.), [Is 55:10](https://ref.ly/Isa55:10).

## צָמַת (id: BDB08219)

\[Str6789] † \[צָמַת] **vb.** put an end to, exterminate, poet. and (in **Qal, Pi.**) hyperb. (prop. *compress,* NH *id., press together;* Ar. صَمَتَ is *be silent,* II, IV.*make speechless, silence;* Syr. ܨܡܰܬ *be silent* (in Lexx));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. חַָ\+יּי …צָֽמְתוּ [La 3:63](https://ref.ly/Lam3:63) \= *they have put an end to my life* (Bu conj. *Pi.*). **Niph.** *be ended, annihilated; Pf.* 1 s. מִפְּנֵיאחשֶׁךְ [Jb 23:17](https://ref.ly/Job23:17) *I am* \[not] *annihilated because of* (the) *darkness;* 3 pl. נִצְמָתוּ [Jb 6:17](https://ref.ly/Job6:17) (of dried\-up brooks). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. sf. צִמְּתַתְנִי [Psalm 119:139](https://ref.ly/Ps119:139) קִנְאָתִי *hath put an end to me* (it is so intense). **Pi‘lēl** *Pf.* 3 pl. sf. צִמְּתֻתוּנִי (but rd. צִמְּתוּנִי Ges§ 55 d, or צִמְּתָתְנִי) Hi \[Ges§ 145k]; cf. Bae) [Psalm 88:17](https://ref.ly/Ps88:17) thine alarms *have annihilated me.* **Hiph.** *Pf.* 2 ms. הִצְמַתָּה [Psalm 73:27](https://ref.ly/Ps73:27); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יַצְמִיתֵם [Psalm 94:23](https://ref.ly/Ps94:23), [94:23](https://ref.ly/Ps94:23); 2 ms. תַּצְמִית [Psalm 143:12](https://ref.ly/Ps143:12), etc.; *Imv.* ms. sf. הַצְמִיתֵם [Psalm 54:7](https://ref.ly/Ps54:7); *Pt.* pl. sf. מַצְמִיתַי [Psalm 69:5](https://ref.ly/Ps69:5); —*exterminate, annihilate,* c. acc. Pers., subj. ## צָעַן (id: BDB08255)

\[Str6813] † \[צָעַן] **vb.** wander, travel (Ar.ظَعَنَ *journey, go away;* prob. connected with As, *ṣênu,* Eth. ጸዐነ፡, Aram. טְעַן, Palm. טען, all *load* (beast, etc.) \= Heb. טָעַן † [Gn 45:17](https://ref.ly/Gen45:17); Aram. טְעַן, ܛܥܰܢ also *bear, carry*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִצְעָן [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20) *travel,* i. e. be removed, of tent (\+ יִסַּע).

> \[Str6814] † צֹ֫עַן **n.pr.loc.** Τανις: Tanis, in Egypt (Egypt. *Ḏa‘nĕ (t)* SteindBAS600, in As. *Ṣi’inu, Sa’anu* Ibid.598 ff. DlPa 315\); it was built 7 years after Hebron acc. to [Nu 13:22](https://ref.ly/Num13:22); [Is 19:11](https://ref.ly/Isa19:11), [19:13](https://ref.ly/Isa19:13), [30:4](https://ref.ly/Isa30:4), Ez [30:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:14), [Psalm 78:12](https://ref.ly/Ps78:12), [78:43](https://ref.ly/Ps78:43) mod. *Ṣân,* in NW. part of Delta; v.also EbGS 5l2 ff. BDLower Eg.3(1895\), 228 PetrieTanis(1885\).

> \[Str6815] † צַעֲנַנִּים **n.pr.loc.** only c. בְּ, pointed as prep., and so most (but ב perh. radical, v. GFM); on border of Naphtali, near Ḳedesh;—´בְּח [Jos 19:33](https://ref.ly/Josh19:33) (Βεσεμεειν, Σεενανειμ, etc.) \+ Ju [4:11](https://ref.ly/Judg4:11) Qr (Kt בצענים), LXX πλεονεκτούντων (√ בָּצַע), ἀναπαυομένων.

* **Associated Passages:** Joshua 19:33; Psalms 78:43; Isaiah 19:11; Numbers 13:22; Isaiah 19:13; Judges 4:11; Ezekiel 30:14; Genesis 45:17; Isaiah 33:20; Isaiah 30:4; Psalms 78:12


## צעף (id: BDB08258)

[Ex 5:8](https://ref.ly/Exod5:8) (J), [2 K 4:40](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:40), [6:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:5). **b.** esp. unto ´י: [Gn 4:10](https://ref.ly/Gen4:10), [Ex 8:8](https://ref.ly/Exod8:8) ( \+ עַ־ל דְּבַר rei), [14:10](https://ref.ly/Exod14:10), [14:15](https://ref.ly/Exod14:15), [15:25](https://ref.ly/Exod15:25), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Exod17:4), [22:22](https://ref.ly/Exod22:22) (צָעֹק יִצְעַק), v:[26](https://ref.ly/Exod22:26), [Nu 12:13](https://ref.ly/Num12:13), [20:16](https://ref.ly/Num20:16), [Jos 24:7](https://ref.ly/Josh24:7) (all JE), Ju [4:3](https://ref.ly/Judg4:3), [10:12](https://ref.ly/Judg10:12), [Dt 26:7](https://ref.ly/Deut26:7), [Is 19:20](https://ref.ly/Isa19:20) (\+ מִפְּנֵי rei), [La 2:18](https://ref.ly/Lam2:18), [Ne 9:27](https://ref.ly/Neh9:27), [Psalm 107:6](https://ref.ly/Ps107:6), [107:28](https://ref.ly/Ps107:28); קוֺלִי וְאֶצְעָקָה ´אֶל־א [77:2](https://ref.ly/Ps77:2), cf. [34:18](https://ref.ly/Ps34:18); c.´לי [2 Ch 13:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:14): נֶגְדֱֶָּ [Psalm 88:2](https://ref.ly/Ps88:2). **c.** *cry* unto (אֶל) idol [Is 46:7](https://ref.ly/Isa46:7). **2\.** abs. *cry, cry out,* in distress, need;—[Gn 27:34](https://ref.ly/Gen27:34) (J; c. acc. cogn.), [Dt 22:24](https://ref.ly/Deut22:24), [22:27](https://ref.ly/Deut22:27), [1 S 33:7](https://ref.ly/Isa33:7) (‖בָּכָה), [65:14](https://ref.ly/Isa65:14) (‖הֵילִיל, opp. רָנַן; c. מִן rei), [Je 22:20](https://ref.ly/Jer22:20), [22:20](https://ref.ly/Jer22:20) (‖נָתַן קוֺל), [49:3](https://ref.ly/Jer49:3) (‖הֵילִיל), [Jb 35:12](https://ref.ly/Job35:12); c. obj. חָמָס [Jb 19:7](https://ref.ly/Job19:7) (‖אֲשַׁוַּע). **3\.** *make outcry, clamour,* [Is 42:2](https://ref.ly/Isa42:2) (\+ יִשָּא, יַשְׁמִיעַ קוֺלוֺ ). **Niph.** *be summoned* (i.e. to arms), abs.; *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִּצָּעֵק Ju [7:23](https://ref.ly/Judg7:23), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Judg7:24), [12:1](https://ref.ly/Judg12:1), so 3 mpl. וַיִּצָּֽעֲקוּ [10:17](https://ref.ly/Judg10:17), [2 K 3:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:21), sq. אַחֲרֵי שָׁאוּל \+ acc. loc. [1 S 13:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:4). **Pi.** *Pt.* מְצַעֵק *cry aloud,* in grief, [2 K 2:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:12). **Hiph.** *call together* הַמִּחְפָּה ´וַיַּצְעֵקאֶת־הָעָם אֶל־י [1 S 10:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:17).

[Gn 19:20](https://ref.ly/Gen19:20));—Σηλωρ, LXXL Σιλωρ, but [Gn 13:10](https://ref.ly/Gen13:10), [Je 48:34](https://ref.ly/Jer48:34) Ζογορ(α); on LXX cf. further LagBN 54 f.: city at SE. end of Dead Sea, צֹעַר [Gn 13:10](https://ref.ly/Gen13:10), [14:2](https://ref.ly/Gen14:2), [14:8](https://ref.ly/Gen14:8) (both \= בֶּלַע) [Dt 34:3](https://ref.ly/Deut34:3), [Is 15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5), [Je 48:34](https://ref.ly/Jer48:34); צוֺעַר [Gn 19:22](https://ref.ly/Gen19:22), [19:30](https://ref.ly/Gen19:30), [19:30](https://ref.ly/Gen19:30); c. ה loc., צֹ֫עֲרָה v:[23](https://ref.ly/Gen19:23) \+ [Je 48:4](https://ref.ly/Jer48:4) (for MT צעוריה), LXX Ζαλορα, JDMich Ew Gf Gie al.—(cf. BuhlGeogr. 271\. 274 GASmGeogr.506 f. 678 (App.).

> \[Str6810,4704] † I. צָעִיר **adj.** little, insignificant, young;—abs.צָעִיר [Gn 25:23](https://ref.ly/Gen25:23) \+; f. צְעִירָה [Gn 19:31](https://ref.ly/Gen19:31) \+; צְעִרָה [1 S 9:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:12); mpl. צְעִירִים [Jb 30:1](https://ref.ly/Job30:1); sf. צְעִירֶיהָ [Je 48:4](https://ref.ly/Jer48:4) Qr (Kt צעוריה but v.צֹעַר); צְעִירֵיהֶם [Je 14:3](https://ref.ly/Jer14:3) (Kt צעוריהם), etc.;—**1\.a.** *little,* with idea of *insignificance* Ju [6:15](https://ref.ly/Judg6:15) (‖הַדַּל), cf. [1 S 9:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:21) (‖קְטַנֵּי), [Mi 5:1](https://ref.ly/Mic5:1), [Psalm 68:28](https://ref.ly/Ps68:28), [Is 60:22](https://ref.ly/Isa60:22) (opp. גוי עצום); of horn in vision [Dn 8:9](https://ref.ly/Dan8:9), rd. prob.אחרת צעירה for אחת מִצְּעִירָה , cf. Bev Dr; צְעִירֵי הַצֹּאן [Je 49:20](https://ref.ly/Jer49:20), [50:45](https://ref.ly/Jer50:45) (fig. of helpless captives). **b.** *insignificant, mean,* [Psalm 119:141](https://ref.ly/Ps119:141) (‖נִבְזֶה), [Je 14:3](https://ref.ly/Jer14:3) (as subst.); on [Je 48:4](https://ref.ly/Jer48:4).v. צֹעַר. **2\.** more oft. of age, *young* (usu. c. art. the *younger, youngest,* as subst.), of son (brother), opp. הַבְּכוֺר [Gn 43:33](https://ref.ly/Gen43:33), [48:14](https://ref.ly/Gen48:14) (both J), [Jos 6:26](https://ref.ly/Josh6:26), [1 K 16:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:34), opp. רַב [Gn 25:23](https://ref.ly/Gen25:23) (J), cf. אני ´ח לְיָמִים ואתם יְשִׁישִׁים [Jb 32:6](https://ref.ly/Job32:6); of daughter (sister), opp. הַבְּכִירָה [Gn 19:31](https://ref.ly/Gen19:31), [19:34](https://ref.ly/Gen19:34), [19:35](https://ref.ly/Gen19:35), [19:38](https://ref.ly/Gen19:38)(J) [29:26](https://ref.ly/Gen29:26) (E);—here belongs also צְעַירִים מִמֶּנִּי לימים [Jb 30:1](https://ref.ly/Job30:1), si vera 1\.; GHBWr Siegf Bu del. ממני לימים; LXX ἐλἁχιστοι); put then under **1 b.**

Ex 25–38 (all P): *overlay, stud with* precious stones, 2 acc. [2 Ch 3:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:6); also *plate,* acc. mater. om., [1 K 6:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:15), [2 K 18:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:16), [Ex 25:11](https://ref.ly/Exod25:11), [38:28](https://ref.ly/Exod38:28); acc. dir. obj., om. [1 K 6:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:32), [6:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:35); c. acc. \+ ב mater. v:[15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:15) *overlaid the floor of the house with timbers*. † **Pu.** *Pt.* מְצֻפֶּה [Pr 26:23](https://ref.ly/Prov26:23) impure silver (Toy) *laid over* (עַל) a sherd; pl. מְחֻפִּים זָהָב [Ex 26:32](https://ref.ly/Exod26:32), pillars *over laid with gold.*

> \[Str6690] † צוֺפַח **n.pr.m.** in Asher, [1 Ch 7:35](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:35), צוֺפָח v:[36](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:36), Σωχαθ, Σωφας\[ρ], LXXL Σουφα.

Eur. 185\);—צֹר [2 S 24:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:7) \+ 32 t., צוֺר [1 K 5:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:15) \+ 8 t. (Gi);—city of Hiram, friend of David and Sol. [2 S 5:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:11), [1 K 5:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:15), [9:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:11), [9:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:12), [1 Ch 14:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr14:1), [2 Ch 2:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:2), [2:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:10); of artif. (another view in Che); ´צ also in name of Ph. fotress, ´מִבְצַר־צ [2 S 24:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:7), ´צ ´עִיר מ [Jos 19:29](https://ref.ly/Josh19:29) (P); threatened by proph. [29:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:18), [29:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:18); cf. [Zc 9:2](https://ref.ly/Zech9:2); 29:3, [Jo 4:4](https://ref.ly/Joel4:4), [Psalm 83:8](https://ref.ly/Ps83:8) (´ישְׁבֵי צ); of Tyrians as proselytes [Psalm 87:4](https://ref.ly/Ps87:4) (predict.);—לְצוֺר [Ho 9:13](https://ref.ly/Hos9:13) is prob. corrupt, read לָצוֺד or לָצַיִד LXX We GASm, cf. > \[Str6867] † צָרֶ֫בֶת **n.f.** scab, scar, of a sore (lit. *a burning, scorching*);—cstr. צָרֶבֶת הַשְּׁחִין הִוא [Lv 13:23](https://ref.ly/Lev13:23); הַמִּכְוָה הִוא ´ח v:[28](https://ref.ly/Lev13:28).

> \[Str6868,6891] † צְרֵדָה **n.pr.loc.** home of Jerob. [1 K 11:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:26), Σαρειρα, A Συριδα; v. \[צְרֵרָה] צָרְֽתָן. p. 866

> חרה (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. ضَرَاء (و, ي) of vein, *run blood, bleed,* ضَرْوٌ) an odorif. tree, or its gum, cf. HomA. und A. i. 4, Sab. חַרו SabDenkm83; (Syr. ܨܪܘܐ *fructus pini,* etc. is loan\-word); NH \= BH; as to form cp. Gk. στύραξ (hardly ‖ in meaning, LagM. i. 234, 384, v. Ency. Bib. infr.), cf. LewyFremdw. 41\).

> \[Str6875] † צֳרִי and ([Gn 37:25](https://ref.ly/Gen37:25)) צְרִי(וּ) **n.\[m.]** a kind of balsam, as merchandise [Gn 37:25](https://ref.ly/Gen37:25) (J), Ez [27:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:17), gift [Gn 43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11) (J), medicament [Je 8:22](https://ref.ly/Jer8:22), [46:11](https://ref.ly/Jer46:11), [51:8](https://ref.ly/Jer51:8) (for national disaster, in fig,);—ThDyerM'Lean in Ency. Bib.:Balm think a resin, like (not necess.\=) gum of mastic\-tree, *pistacia lentiscus* (otherwise PostHastings DB).

\[Str6873] † I. צָרַח **vb.** cry, roar (NH *id., cry* (of raven); As. *ṣarâḫu, cry aloud*, Ar. صَرَخَ, Eth. ፀርኅ፡ Targum צְרַח Aph. (of bear), Syr. ܨܪܰܚ esp. Aph. and deriv.);—**Qal** *Pt. act.* מַר צֹרֵחַ שָׁם גִּבּוֺר [Zp 1:14](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:14) *bitterly roareth there a hero*, but abrupt in context and improb.; Gr (in part after LXX) conj. יִצְרַח כַּגִּבּוֺר ´קוֺל י (cf. **Hiph.**). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יַצְרִיחַ [Is 42:13](https://ref.ly/Isa42:13) *utter a roar* (of ´י, going to battle; ‖יָרִיעַ).

> II. חרח (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. ضَرَحَ *dig* a ضَرِيح, also *cleave* the ground, *rend open;* ضَرِيحٌ *sepulchral chamber* (with niches for bodies); Nab. צריחה (cf. Nö in EutNab. Inschr. 55 DrSm. 76\)).

GFM).

## צָרַף (id: BDB08361)

\[Str6884] † צָרַף **vb.** smelt, refine, test (NH *id.;* Ph. מצרף *smelter;* As. *ṣurrupu, refined, ṣarpu, silver, naṣraptu, crucible;* Ar. صِرْفٌ is *pure, unmixed* (esp. of wine); very doubtful is Sab. צרף *silver, money,* cf. MordtmHim. Inschr. pp. 14, 29 CISiv, No. 291, 1\.1\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´ח [Je 6:29](https://ref.ly/Jer6:29), sf. צְרָפָתְהוּ [Psalm 105:19](https://ref.ly/Ps105:19); 2 ms. sf. צְרַפְתָּנִי [Psalm 17:3](https://ref.ly/Ps17:3), etc.; *Impf.* 1 s. אֶצְרֹף [Is 1:25](https://ref.ly/Isa1:25), sf. אֶצְרְפֶנּוּ Ju [7:4](https://ref.ly/Judg7:4); *Imv.* ms. צרופה [Psalm 26:2](https://ref.ly/Ps26:2) Kt, צָרְפָה Qr; *Inf. abs.* צָרוֺף [Je 6:29](https://ref.ly/Jer6:29); *cstr.* לִצְרוֺף [Dn 11:35](https://ref.ly/Dan11:35), etc.; *Pt. act.* sf. צוֺרְכָּם [Je 9:6](https://ref.ly/Jer9:6), etc.; *pass*. צָרוּף [Psalm 12:7](https://ref.ly/Ps12:7), etc.;—**1\.** *smelt, refine:* [Je 6:29](https://ref.ly/Jer6:29), [6:29](https://ref.ly/Jer6:29) *in vain hath he smelted continually* (inf. abs.; fig. of purifying people); so *smelt away* [Is 1:25](https://ref.ly/Isa1:25) (acc. of dross; fig.); of silver [Zc 13:9](https://ref.ly/Zech13:9), [Psalm 66:10](https://ref.ly/Ps66:10), כֶּסֶף צָרוּף בַּעֲלִיל [12:7](https://ref.ly/Ps12:7) (all sim.); of ´אִמְרַת י [Psalm 18:31](https://ref.ly/Ps18:31) \= [2 S 22:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:31), [Psalm 119:140](https://ref.ly/Ps119:140), [Pr 30:5](https://ref.ly/Prov30:5) (אִמְרַת אֱלוֺהַּ); *refine* (men, by trials) [Dn 11:35](https://ref.ly/Dan11:35) (\+ לִבָרֵר, לַלְבֵּן). **2\.** *test* Ju [7:4](https://ref.ly/Judg7:4) *I will test them* (the warriors) *for thee* (´י subj.);—perh. also v:[3](https://ref.ly/Judg7:3) (v. I. צפר); more gen., of ´י's *testing, trying* (the hearts of) men (oft. ‖בָּחַן) [Je 9:6](https://ref.ly/Jer9:6), [Is 48:10](https://ref.ly/Isa48:10), [Zc 13:9](https://ref.ly/Zech13:9), [Psalm 26:2](https://ref.ly/Ps26:2), [66:10](https://ref.ly/Ps66:10). **3\.** *test* (and prove true) a man (subj. ´י's saying) [Psalm 105:19](https://ref.ly/Ps105:19). **4\.** *Pt. act.* as **n.** *smelter, refiner,* hence \= *goldsmith* Ju [17:4](https://ref.ly/Judg17:4), [Je 10:9](https://ref.ly/Jer10:9), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Jer10:14), [51:17](https://ref.ly/Jer51:17), [Is 40:19](https://ref.ly/Isa40:19), [40:19](https://ref.ly/Isa40:19) (but perhaps del. vb, cf. Du Che Marti), [41:7](https://ref.ly/Isa41:7), [46:6](https://ref.ly/Isa46:6), [Pr 25:4](https://ref.ly/Prov25:4); as a guild [Ne 3:8](https://ref.ly/Neh3:8), [3:32](https://ref.ly/Neh3:32). **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִצָּֽרְפוּ [Dn 12:10](https://ref.ly/Dan12:10) many *shall be refined* (by suffering; \+ יִתְבָּֽרְרוּ, יִתְלַבְּנוּ). **Pi.** *Pt.* מְצָרֵף as **n.** *a refiner,* ´כְּאֵשׁ מ [Mal 3:2](https://ref.ly/Mal3:2) (sim. of ´י), cf. ´מְ alone v:[3](https://ref.ly/Mal3:3) (\+ טְטַהֵר כֶּסֶף).

> \[Str6885] † צֹרְפִי **n.\[m.] coll.** goldsmiths;—only ´בֶּן־הַצּ [Ne 3:31](https://ref.ly/Neh3:31) i.e. *belonging to the goldsmiths,* a member of their guild.

> \[Str6886] † צָֽרְפַת **n.pr.loc.** Σαρεπτα: on coast S. of Sidon (? *smelting*\-place; on strange form cf. LagBN 84; As. *Ṣariptu* DlPa 284 COT:[1 K 17:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:9); Egypt. *Ḏa\-ïra\-pu\-ti* WMMAs. u. Eur. 184\);—´ח Ob:[20](https://ref.ly/Obad1:20); צָֽרְפָ֫תָה [1 K 17:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:9), [17:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:10) (so Gi; פַ֫תָה\- van d. H. Baer); mod. *Ṣarfend* RobBR ii. 474 ff. PietschmPhön. 58 f..

> \[Str4715] † מַצְרֵף **n.\[m.]** crucible (prop. *place* or *instrument of refining*); לַכֶּסֶף וְכוּר לַזָּהָב ´מ [Pr 17:3](https://ref.ly/Prov17:3), [27:21](https://ref.ly/Prov27:21).

## I צָרַר (id: BDB08365)

\[Str3334,6887] † I. צָרַר **vb.** bind, tie up, be restricted, narrow, scant, cramped (NH *id.;* Ar. صَرَّ *bind, tie up;* so Aram. צְרַר, ܨܰܪ);—**A.** trans.: **Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. צָרַר [Ho 4:19](https://ref.ly/Hos4:19), [Pr 30:4](https://ref.ly/Prov30:4); *Imv.* צוֺר [Is 8:16](https://ref.ly/Isa8:16); *Inf. cstr.* צְרוֺר [Pr 26:8](https://ref.ly/Prov26:8) (?); *Pt. act.* צֹרֵר [Jb 26:8](https://ref.ly/Job26:8); *pass.* צָרוּר [Ho 13:12](https://ref.ly/Hos13:12), f. צְרוּרָה [1 S 25:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:29); fpl. צְרֻרֹת [Ex 12:34](https://ref.ly/Exod12:34), [2 S 20:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:3); —*bind* or *tie up,* of kneading\-troughs [Ex 12:34](https://ref.ly/Exod12:34) (E); fig. of a life preserved by ´י, נֶפֶשׁ צְרוּרָה בִּצְרוֺר הַחַיִּים [1 S 25:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:29); of preserving prophetic teaching [Is 8:16](https://ref.ly/Isa8:16); of retention of guilt [Ho 13:12](https://ref.ly/Hos13:12); of ´י's *binding* waters in (a garment of) cloud [Pr 30:4](https://ref.ly/Prov30:4), [Jb 26:8](https://ref.ly/Job26:8); \= *shut up* [2 S 20:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:3); —צָרַר רוּחַ אֹתָהּ בִּכְנָפֶיהָ [Ho 4:19](https://ref.ly/Hos4:19) (si vera l.) pregn., *the wind hath wrapped her up in its wings,* to carry her off; כִּצְרוֺר אֶבֶן [Pr 26:8](https://ref.ly/Prov26:8) *like the tying up of a stone,* but unintellig. in context (v. Toy). **Pu.** *Pt.* pl. מְצֹרָרִים [Jos 9:4](https://ref.ly/Josh9:4) *tied up* (mended by tying), of old wine\-skins. **B.** intrans.: **Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. צָ֫רָה [Is 28:20](https://ref.ly/Isa28:20); *Impf.* 3 ms. יֵצַר [Pr 4:12](https://ref.ly/Prov4:12), יֵ֫צֶר לוֺ [Jb 20:22](https://ref.ly/Job20:22), וַ֫יֵּצֶד [Gn 32:8](https://ref.ly/Gen32:8) \+, etc.;—*be scant, cramped, in straits;* of scanty bed\-covering [Is 28:20](https://ref.ly/Isa28:20) (in fig.); of land too small for (מִן) its people [Is 49:19](https://ref.ly/Isa49:19) \+ [Jos 19:47](https://ref.ly/Josh19:47) (for וַיֵּצֵא) DrExpos. Jan. 1887, 59 Benn, cf. LXX; of steps \= *be cramped,* or impeded, [Pr 4:12](https://ref.ly/Prov4:12), [Jb 18:7](https://ref.ly/Job18:7) (both in fig.); esp. impers. c. ל Pers., וַיֵּצֶר לוֺ [Gn 32:8](https://ref.ly/Gen32:8) *and it was narrow for him* \= he was in straits, distress, so Ju [2:15](https://ref.ly/Judg2:15), [2 S 13:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:2), [Jb 20:22](https://ref.ly/Job20:22), also 3 fs. וַתֵּ֫צֶר לְ Ju [10:9](https://ref.ly/Judg10:9), [1 S 30:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:6) (v. Dr). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. וְהֵצַר [Dt 28:52](https://ref.ly/Deut28:52), [28:52](https://ref.ly/Deut28:52) consec.; 1 s. וַהֲצֵרֹתִי [Je 10:18](https://ref.ly/Jer10:18), [Zp 1:17](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:17); *Impf.* 3 ms. יָצַר־לוֺ [1 K 8:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:37) \= [2 Ch 6:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:28), וַיָּ֫צַד [2 Ch 28:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:20); 3 mpl. וַיָּצֵרוּ [Ne 9:27](https://ref.ly/Neh9:27); *Inf. cstr.* הָצֵר [2 Ch 28:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:22), [33:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:12);—*make narrow* for, *press hard* upon, *cause distress* to, c. ל Pers., [Je 10:18](https://ref.ly/Jer10:18) (subj. ´י), [Zp 1:17](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:17) (subj. *id.*); [Dt 28:52](https://ref.ly/Deut28:52), [28:52](https://ref.ly/Deut28:52), [1 K 8:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:37) \= [2 Ch 6:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:28), [Ne 9:27](https://ref.ly/Neh9:27) (all subj. foe), [2 Ch 28:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:20) (subj. TP); indef. subj. [2 Ch 28:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:22) (but rd. כִּי אִם לְצָרָה לוֺ, and join to v:[21](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:21), opp. לְעֶזְרָה לוֺ, LXX Kit BuhlLex), [33:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:12).

> \[Str6862] † I. צַר **adj.** narrow, tight;—abs. ´ח [2 K 6:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:1) \+; צָר [Nu 22:26](https://ref.ly/Num22:26) \+; f. צָרָה [Pr 23:27](https://ref.ly/Prov23:27); —*narrow,* מָקוֺם צָר [Nu 22:26](https://ref.ly/Num22:26), צַר מִמֶּנּוּ [2 K 6:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:1) *too narrow for us,* cf. צַר־לִי הַמָּקוֺם [Is 49:20](https://ref.ly/Isa49:20) (of land of Judah after exile); בְּאֵר צָרָה [Pr 23:27](https://ref.ly/Prov23:27) (fig. of harlot; i.e. rescue difficult; ‖שׁוּחָה עֲמֻקָּה); כַּנָּהָר צָר [Is 59:19](https://ref.ly/Isa59:19) *like a contracted* (and hence swift, powerful) *river* (sim. of ´י; Klo נְהַר מָצוֺר, CheHpt מִצֹּר ´נ, both ‘of Egypt’); סָגוּר חוֺתָם צָר [Jb 41:7](https://ref.ly/Job41:7) (of scaly back of crocodile), usu. closely joined (as) *with tight seal* (LXX Me BI Hoffm Bu Du חוֺתַם צֹר *with seal of flint,* but why specify material?); בְּיוֺם צָרָה צַר כֹּחֶָלָה [Pr 24:10](https://ref.ly/Prov24:10) *thy strength* will be *narrow,* limited (si vera l., cf. Toy).

## צֹר (id: BDB08378)

\[Str6864] † II.. צֹר **n. \[m.]** id.;—´ח used as knife [Ex 4:25](https://ref.ly/Exod4:25); in comp., ´כְּשָׁמִיר חָזָק מִצ Ez [3:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:9); pl. חַרְכוֺת צֻרִים [Jos 5:2](https://ref.ly/Josh5:2), [5:3](https://ref.ly/Josh5:3) *knives of flint.*—צזּר חַרְכּוֺ [Psalm 89:44](https://ref.ly/Ps89:44) is dub., ´ח usu. taken as \= צֹר, and then either *flint of his sword* (i.e. sword sharp as flint, Bae), or *edge* (like flint) *of his sword* (most); We leaves untransl.; הַצֻּדִים v. I. חֶלְקָה—[Jb 22:24](https://ref.ly/Job22:24) v.

## I צוּר (id: BDB08379)

\[Str6697,6865] I. צוּר sub V. צור. I. צֹר v. p. 862\. p. 849

> \[Str6872] † II. צְרוֺר **n. m.** pebble;—´ח [2 S 17:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:13); also [Am 9:9](https://ref.ly/Amos9:9) (where perhaps fig. for grain of wheat, in metaph. of winnowed Isr., cf. Dr Now al.; \> pebble retained in sieve PreuschemZAW xv (1895\), 24, cf. on custom WetzstZPV xiv (1891\), f.). p. 865

> \[Str6872] † III. צְרוֺר **n. pr. m.** grandfather of Ḳish [1 S 9:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:1), Αρεδ, LXX of L Σαρα.

> \[Str6888,6868,6891] † \[צְרֵרָה] **n. pr. loc.** צְרֵרָ֫תָה Ju [7:22](https://ref.ly/Judg7:22) (Γαραγαθα, LXX. of L καὶ ἦν συνηγμένη), usu. thought corrupt for צְרֵדָ֫תָה, and in nay case prob. \= צָֽרְתָן, q.v. (GFM).

> \[Str6889] † צֶ֫רֶת **n.pr.m.** in Judah [1 Ch 4:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:7), Αρεθ, LXX of L Σαρηθ.

> \[Str6890] † צֶרֶת הַשַּׁ֫חַר **n.pr.loc.** assigned to Reuben [Jos 13:19](https://ref.ly/Josh13:19), Σεραδα και Σειων, LXX of L Σαρθ; cf. mod. *eṣ\-Sara,* on spur of Mt. *'Aṭṭârûs*, E. of Dead Sea BuhlGeogr. 268\.

> \[Str6891,6868,6888,6868] † צָֽרְתָן **n.pr.loc.** in Jordan valley, near ford, BuhlGeogr. 181; exact site known; [Jos 3:16](https://ref.ly/Josh3:16) (Καθιαιρειν, LXX of L Καριαθιαρειμ), [1 K 7:46](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:46) (Σειρα, LXX of L Σαρθαν) \= צְרֵדָ֫תָה [2 Ch 4:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:17) (Αναμε \[i.e. ἀνὰ μέσον] σιρδαθαι; Σα(ρι)δαθα; is Chr right?); צָֽרְחַ֫נָה [1 K 4:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:12) (Σεσαθαν, LXX of L Σαρθαν)—cf. צְרֵרָתָה Ju [7:22](https://ref.ly/Judg7:22), appar. same place; identity with צְרֵדָה [1 K 11:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:26) is usually assumed, but not proven.

> ק, *Ḳôph*, 19th letter; \= 100 in post B. Heb.

> \[Str6892] \[קֵא] v. קיא. קָאם v. קום.

> \[Str6893] † קָאַת, קָאָת **n.\[f.]** a bird, usu. pelican (cf. LXX Lv Dt Psalm, Vulgate Psalm (der. by Thes from \[קוא], קיא (v. Köii. 1, 173, and, on ת, Ges§ 80 g), as *throwing up* food from its crop for its young); but sea\-fowl improb. in Psalm Is; NH קָאָת, Targum קָאתָא *id.*);—abs. הַקָּאָת [Lv 11:18](https://ref.ly/Lev11:18) \= [Dt 14:17](https://ref.ly/Deut14:17), as unclean; קָאַת (van d. H. Gi; Baer קָאָת) [Is 34:11](https://ref.ly/Isa34:11), [Zp 2:14](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:14), as inhabiting ruins; cstr. קְאַת מִדְכָּר [Psalm 102:7](https://ref.ly/Ps102:7), sim. of loneliness.
